   <section class="manifesto block-pad" id="manifesto">
    <div class="section-header">
     <span class="micro">
      O QUE NOS MOVE
     </span>
     <span class="line">
     </span>
     <span class="micro">
      UM NOVO OLHAR
     </span>
    </div>
    <div class="manifesto-layout">
     <h2>
      A CIDADE
      <br/>
      NÃO É SÓ
      <br/>
      <span>
       CONCRETO.
      </span>
     </h2>
     <div class="manifesto-right">
      <div aria-hidden="true" class="ring-icon">
       ✳
      </div>
      <p>
       É encontro. É movimento. É a experiência de quem atravessa cada rua todos os dias.
      </p>
      <p>
       O
       <strong>
        URBS
       </strong>
       transforma observações sobre a infraestrutura em informações localizadas. Uma plataforma acadêmica para enxergar o território de forma mais clara.
      </p>

   // Rodar antes da pintura da página para manter continuidade visual entre telas.
    try {
      const started = Number(sessionStorage.getItem('urbs-arrival-v8'));
      sessionStorage.removeItem('urbs-arrival-v8');
      if (started && Date.now() - started < 15000 && !matchMedia('(prefers-reduced-motion: reduce)').matches) {
        document.documentElement.classList.add('urbs-is-arriving');
      }
    } catch (_) {}

}

  scroll-behavior: smooth;
}

}

    transform: none !important;
    min-height: auto;
    padding: 30px 24px 28px;
    border-radius: 24px;
  }
  .journey-cta {
    width: 100%;
    justify-content: space-between;
  }
}

===== ARQUIVO: public\assets\js\api.js =====
(() => {
  "use strict";

  const apiBase = new URL("../backend/public/api/", document.baseURI);

  async function request(path, options = {}) {
    const headers = new Headers(options.headers || {});
    const init = {
      method: options.method || "GET",
      credentials: "same-origin",
      headers,
    };

    if (options.body !== undefined) {
      headers.set("Content-Type", "application/json; charset=utf-8");
      init.body = JSON.stringify(options.body);
    }

    let response;
    try {
      response = await fetch(new URL(path, apiBase), init);
    } catch (error) {
      throw new Error("Não foi possível conectar ao backend. Confira se o Apache e o MySQL estão ativos no XAMPP.");
    }

    let payload;
    try {
      payload = await response.json();
    } catch (error) {
      throw new Error("O backend retornou uma resposta inválida.");
    }

    if (!response.ok || payload.success === false) {
      const error = new Error(payload.message || "Não foi possível concluir a solicitação.");
      error.status = response.status;
      error.payload = payload;
      throw error;
    }

    return payload;
  }

  window.UrbsAPI = Object.freeze({
    base: apiBase,
    request,
    session: () => request("auth/session.php"),
    logout: () => request("auth/logout.php", { method: "POST" }),
  });
})();


===== ARQUIVO: public\assets\js\auth-globe.js =====
/* URBS V6 — Globo satélite real, inspirado na experiência de navegação Earth.
 * CesiumJS + Esri World Imagery, sem copiar imagens/código do Google Earth.
 * Mantenha as atribuições do provedor visíveis na cena.
 * Login e cadastro usam a API PHP do projeto.
 */
(() => {
    'use strict';
    const root = document.querySelector('.auth-experience');
    if (!root)
        return;
    const C = window.Cesium;
    const gsap = window.gsap;
    const reduced = matchMedia('(prefers-reduced-motion: reduce)').matches;
    const host = document.getElementById('planet-canvas');
    const status = document.querySelector('.screen-reader-status');
    const planetZone = document.querySelector('.planet-zone');
    const loginSide = document.getElementById('login-side');
    const registerSide = document.getElementById('register-side');
    const WORLD_IMAGERY = 'https://services.arcgisonline.com/ArcGIS/rest/services/World_Imagery/MapServer';
    let mode = 'login', entering = false, viewer = null, raf = 0, lastTime = 0;
    let pointerX = 0, pointerY = 0, panX = 0, panY = 0, switching = false;
    let dragging = false, dragX = 0, dragY = 0, lastDrag = 0;
    const globe = { rotation: { y: 0 } }; // Proxy animável pelo GSAP; vira longitude da câmera Cesium.
    const initialCenter = { lon: 23, lat: 15 }; // África e Oriente Médio.
    function fallback(message) {
        host?.classList.add('globe-fallback');
        if (status)
            status.textContent = message || 'Globo de satélite indisponível; formulários disponíveis.';
    }
    function resizeGlobe() {
        if (!viewer)
            return;
        const touch = matchMedia('(pointer: coarse)').matches;
        const pixels = Math.max(1, host.clientWidth * host.clientHeight);
        const ratio = window.devicePixelRatio || 1;
        const pixelBudget = touch ? 1800000 : 3800000;
        const targetRatio = touch ? Math.min(ratio, 1.7) : Math.min(ratio * 1.3, 2);
        viewer.resolutionScale = Math.min(targetRatio, Math.sqrt(pixelBudget / pixels)) / ratio;
        globeCamera();
    }
    function globeCamera() {
        if (!viewer || viewer.isDestroyed() || entering)
            return;
        const mobile = innerWidth <= 760;
        const lon = initialCenter.lon + C.Math.toDegrees(globe.rotation.y) + panX * 2.0;
        const lat = initialCenter.lat + panY * 1.3;
        // Câmera acima do centro geográfico da Terra. O movimento da longitude
        // equivale à rotação do planeta, preservando relevo e geografia reais.
        const height = mobile ? 15200000 : 13100000;
        viewer.camera.setView({
            destination: C.Cartesian3.fromDegrees(lon, lat, height),
            orientation: { heading: 0, pitch: -C.Math.PI_OVER_TWO, roll: 0 }
        });
    }
    async function loadGlobeImagery(currentViewer) {
        const addLayer = (provider, index) => {
            if (!currentViewer.isDestroyed())
                currentViewer.imageryLayers.addImageryProvider(provider, index);
        };
        // O mosaico local aparece enquanto o satélite carrega e cobre falhas de rede.
        const results = await Promise.allSettled([
            C.SingleTileImageryProvider.fromUrl('assets/textures/earth-day.jpg', {
                credit: 'NASA / Earth Observatory · Blue Marble'
            }).then(provider => addLayer(provider, 0)),
            C.ArcGisMapServerImageryProvider.fromUrl(WORLD_IMAGERY, {
                enablePickFeatures: false
            }).then(provider => addLayer(provider))
        ]);
        if (results.every(result => result.status === 'rejected') && viewer === currentViewer) {
            currentViewer.destroy();
            viewer = null;
            fallback();
        }
    }
    async function addOceanReflection(currentViewer) {
        const mask = new Image();
        mask.src = 'assets/textures/ocean-mask.png';
        try {
            await mask.decode();
        }
        catch {
            return;
        }
        if (currentViewer.isDestroyed())
            return;

        // A direção da luz acompanha a câmera, inclusive no lado oposto do globo.
        const material = new C.Material({
            fabric: {
                uniforms: { oceanMask: mask, reflectionStrength: 0 },
                source: `
                    czm_material czm_getMaterial(czm_materialInput inputData) {
                        czm_material material = czm_getDefaultMaterial(inputData);
                        vec3 worldPosition = (czm_inverseView3D * vec4(-inputData.positionToEyeEC, 1.0)).xyz;
                        vec3 surface = normalize(worldPosition * vec3(1.0, 1.0, 1.0067395));
                        vec2 uv = vec2(atan(surface.y, surface.x) / czm_twoPi + .5, asin(clamp(surface.z, -1.0, 1.0)) / czm_pi + .5);
                        float water = smoothstep(.2, .8, texture(oceanMask, uv).r);

                        vec3 sun = normalize(czm_inverseViewRotation3D * vec3(-.99, -.12, .045));
                        vec3 eye = normalize(czm_inverseViewRotation3D * inputData.positionToEyeEC);
                        vec3 halfAngle = normalize(sun + eye);
                        float alignment = max(dot(surface, halfAngle), 0.0);
                        float facing = max(dot(surface, eye), 0.0);
                        float daylight = smoothstep(0.0, .22, dot(surface, sun));

                        vec3 east = normalize(vec3(-surface.y, surface.x, .0001));
                        vec3 north = normalize(cross(surface, east));
                        float eastOffset = dot(east, halfAngle);
                        float crossSpread = eastOffset * eastOffset;
                        float body = pow(alignment, 53.0) * exp(-12.0 * crossSpread);
                        float halo = pow(alignment, 12.0) * exp(-4.0 * crossSpread);

                        // As ondas alteram a orientação da água. Suavize o detalhe subpixel.
                        vec3 waves = vec3(
                            dot(surface, vec3(122.0, -78.0, 248.0)),
                            dot(surface, vec3(68.0, 123.0, 171.0)),
                            dot(surface, vec3(181.0, 61.0, -94.0))
                        );
                        vec3 footprint = fwidth(waves);
                        float detail = 1.0 - smoothstep(.5, 1.55, max(footprint.x, max(footprint.y, footprint.z)));
                        float eastWave = .023 * sin(waves.x + .8 * sin(waves.y)) + .012 * cos(waves.z);
                        float northWave = .018 * sin(waves.y + .7 * cos(waves.z)) + .010 * cos(waves.x);
                        vec3 waveNormal = normalize(surface + detail * (eastWave * east + northWave * north));
                        float sparkle = pow(max(dot(waveNormal, halfAngle), 0.0), 158.0);

                        // Fresnel da água e compressão gradual dos brilhos mais intensos.
                        float fresnel = .02 + .98 * pow(1.0 - clamp(dot(eye, halfAngle), 0.0, 1.0), 5.0);
                        float fresnelGain = min(1.3, .9 + 2.4 * fresnel);
                        float sheen = .12 * pow(facing, .85);
                        float energy = sheen + daylight * fresnelGain * (1.20 * sparkle + .62 * body + .28 * halo);
                        float visible = smoothstep(0.0, .18, facing);
                        float tint = smoothstep(.03, .65, body + .3 * halo);
                        material.diffuse = mix(vec3(.48, .66, .85), vec3(1.0, .97, .88), tint);
                        material.alpha = water * .94 * (1.0 - exp(-reflectionStrength * energy)) * visible;
                        return material;
                    }
                `
            }
        });
        currentViewer.scene.globe.material = material;

        // A textura é enviada à GPU pelo Cesium após o material entrar em uso.
        let frames = 0;
        const removeListener = currentViewer.scene.postRender.addEventListener(() => {
            if (++frames === 2) {
                material.uniforms.reflectionStrength = 1.0;
                removeListener();
            }
        });
    }
    function initGlobe() {
        if (!C || !host) {
            fallback();
            return;
        }
        try {
            // CesiumJS é usado apenas como renderizador. Sem controles ou interface de mapa.
            // O usuário vê um planeta limpo, preservando créditos de imagem obrigatórios.
            viewer = new C.Viewer(host, {
                animation: false, timeline: false, fullscreenButton: false,
                homeButton: false, navigationHelpButton: false, sceneModePicker: false,
                baseLayerPicker: false, geocoder: false, infoBox: false,
                selectionIndicator: false, baseLayer: false,
                terrainProvider: new C.EllipsoidTerrainProvider(),
                skyBox: false, sun: false, moon: false,
                contextOptions: { webgl: { alpha: true } },
                useBrowserRecommendedResolution: false,
                msaaSamples: matchMedia('(pointer: coarse)').matches ? 2 : 4,
                requestRenderMode: false, shouldAnimate: true
            });
            viewer.scene.globe.enableLighting = false;
            viewer.scene.globe.show = true;
            viewer.scene.globe.baseColor = C.Color.fromCssColorString('#123049');
            viewer.scene.globe.maximumScreenSpaceError = matchMedia('(pointer: coarse)').matches ? 1.6 : 1.15;
            viewer.scene.backgroundColor = C.Color.TRANSPARENT;
            viewer.scene.skyAtmosphere.show = true;
            viewer.scene.fog.enabled = false;
            viewer.scene.screenSpaceCameraController.enableInputs = false;
            viewer.scene.globe.showGroundAtmosphere = true;
            resizeGlobe();
            window.addEventListener('resize', resizeGlobe);
            void loadGlobeImagery(viewer);
            void addOceanReflection(viewer);
            lastTime = performance.now();
            function animate(now) {
                if (!viewer || entering) {
                    raf = 0;
                    return;
                }
                raf = requestAnimationFrame(animate);
                const dt = Math.min(.06, (now - lastTime) / 1000 || 0);
                lastTime = now;
                if (!switching && !dragging && !reduced && now - lastDrag > 2200)
                    globe.rotation.y += .014 * dt; // Reinicia ao soltar / ficar inativo.
                panX += (pointerX - panX) * Math.min(1, dt * 1.6);
                panY += (pointerY - panY) * Math.min(1, dt * 1.6);
                globeCamera();
            }
            raf = requestAnimationFrame(animate);
            document.addEventListener('visibilitychange', () => {
                if (document.hidden) {
                    cancelAnimationFrame(raf);
                    raf = 0;
                }
                else if (!raf && viewer && !entering) {
                    lastTime = performance.now();
                    raf = requestAnimationFrame(animate);
                }
            });
            window.addEventListener('pagehide', event => {
                if (event.persisted)
                    return;
                cancelAnimationFrame(raf);
                window.removeEventListener('resize', resizeGlobe);
                if (viewer && !viewer.isDestroyed())
                    viewer.destroy();
                viewer = null;
            });
        }
        catch (err) {
            console.warn('URBS: CesiumJS indisponível.', err);
            fallback();
        }
    }
    // Arrastar no globo altera a longitude e latitude em tempo real. O globo
    // retoma sua rotação lenta após 2,2 s sem interação; nenhum controle Cesium interfere.
    planetZone.addEventListener('pointerdown', e => {
        if (entering || switching || e.button !== 0 || e.target.closest('.cesium-viewer-bottom'))
            return;
        dragging = true;
        dragX = e.clientX;
        dragY = e.clientY;
        lastDrag = performance.now();
        planetZone.classList.add('is-dragging');
        try {
            planetZone.setPointerCapture(e.pointerId);
        }
        catch (_) { }
    });
    planetZone.addEventListener('pointermove', e => {
        if (!dragging || entering)
            return;
        const dx = e.clientX - dragX, dy = e.clientY - dragY;
        globe.rotation.y -= dx * .006;
        panY = Math.max(-26, Math.min(26, panY + dy * .12));
        pointerY = panY;
        dragX = e.clientX;
        dragY = e.clientY;
        lastDrag = performance.now();
        globeCamera();
    });
    const stopDrag = () => { dragging = false; lastDrag = performance.now(); planetZone.classList.remove('is-dragging'); };
    planetZone.addEventListener('pointerup', stopDrag);
    planetZone.addEventListener('pointercancel', stopDrag);
    planetZone.addEventListener('lostpointercapture', stopDrag);
    document.addEventListener('pointermove', event => {
        if (reduced || entering || event.pointerType === 'touch')
            return;
        pointerX = (event.clientX / innerWidth - .5) * 2;
        if (!dragging)
            pointerY = (event.clientY / innerHeight - .5) * 2;
    }, { passive: true });
    // Alternância exatamente em 1,2 s: globo muda de metade + longitude da câmera.
    function switchMode(next) {
        if (entering || next === mode || switching)
            return;
        const toRegister = next === 'register';
        const outgoing = toRegister ? loginSide : registerSide;
        const incoming = toRegister ? registerSide : loginSide;
        const mobile = matchMedia('(max-width:760px)').matches;
        mode = next;
        root.dataset.mode = next;
        outgoing.inert = true;
        outgoing.setAttribute('aria-hidden', 'true');
        incoming.inert = false;
        incoming.removeAttribute('aria-hidden');
        incoming.style.pointerEvents = 'auto';
        outgoing.style.pointerEvents = 'none';
        const done = () => {
            incoming.style.visibility = 'visible';
            incoming.style.opacity = '1';
            incoming.style.transform = 'translateX(0px)';
            outgoing.style.visibility = 'hidden';
            switching = false;
        };
        if (!gsap || reduced) {
            planetZone.style.left = mobile ? '0%' : toRegister ? '0%' : '50%';
            globe.rotation.y += toRegister ? -1.7 : 1.7;
            outgoing.style.visibility = 'hidden';
            outgoing.style.opacity = '0';
            incoming.style.visibility = 'visible';
            done();
            globeCamera();
        }
        else {
            gsap.killTweensOf([planetZone, outgoing, incoming, globe.rotation]);
            switching = true;
            const tl = gsap.timeline({ onComplete: done });
            tl.to(outgoing, { autoAlpha: 0, x: toRegister ? -85 : 85, duration: .48, ease: 'power2.in' }, 0)
                .set(incoming, { visibility: 'visible', opacity: 0, x: toRegister ? 85 : -85 }, .35)
                .to(incoming, { autoAlpha: 1, x: 0, duration: .75, ease: 'power3.out' }, .45);
            if (!mobile)
                tl.to(planetZone, { left: toRegister ? '0%' : '50%', duration: 1.2, ease: 'power3.inOut' }, 0);
            tl.to(globe.rotation, { y: globe.rotation.y + (toRegister ? -1.7 : 1.7), duration: 1.2, ease: 'power2.inOut', onUpdate: globeCamera }, 0);
        }
        if (status)
            status.textContent = toRegister ? 'Cadastro à direita. Terra à esquerda.' : 'Login à esquerda. Terra à direita.';
    }
    document.querySelectorAll('[data-switch]').forEach(btn => btn.addEventListener('click', () => {
        const next = btn.dataset.switch;
        switchMode(next);
        setTimeout(() => document.getElementById(next === 'login' ? 'login-email' : 'register-name')?.focus({ preventScroll: true }), reduced ? 0 : 1250);
    }));
    document.querySelectorAll('.show-password').forEach(btn => btn.addEventListener('click', () => {
        const field = document.getElementById(btn.dataset.target);
        const show = field.type === 'password';
        field.type = show ? 'text' : 'password';
        btn.textContent = show ? 'OCULTAR' : 'MOSTRAR';
        btn.setAttribute('aria-label', show ? 'Ocultar senha' : 'Mostrar senha');
    }));
    async function enterExperience(event) {
        event.preventDefault();
        if (entering)
            return;
        const form = event.currentTarget, error = form.querySelector('.field-error');
        error.textContent = '';
        if (!form.checkValidity()) {
            const field = form.querySelector(':invalid');
            error.textContent = field?.validity.typeMismatch ? 'Digite um e-mail válido.' : field?.validity.tooShort ? 'Campo com menos caracteres do que o mínimo exigido.' : 'Preencha todos os campos obrigatórios.';
            field?.focus();
            return;
        }
        if (form.id === 'register-form') {
            const pass = document.getElementById('register-password');
            const confirm = document.getElementById('register-confirm');
            if (pass.value !== confirm.value) {
                error.textContent = 'As senhas não coincidem.';
                confirm.focus();
                return;
            }
        }
        const button = form.querySelector('.submit-button');
        const originalLabel = button?.textContent;
        if (button) {
            button.disabled = true;
            button.textContent = 'CONECTANDO…';
        }
        document.querySelectorAll('.switch-link').forEach(item => item.disabled = true);
        try {
            if (!window.UrbsAPI)
                throw new Error('A conexão com a API não foi iniciada. Atualize a página.');
            const register = form.id === 'register-form';
            const payload = register
                ? {
                    name: document.getElementById('register-name').value.trim(),
                    email: document.getElementById('register-email').value.trim(),
                    password: document.getElementById('register-password').value
                }
                : {
                    email: document.getElementById('login-email').value.trim(),
                    password: document.getElementById('login-password').value
                };
            const result = await window.UrbsAPI.request(
                register ? 'auth/register.php' : 'auth/login.php',
                { method: 'POST', body: payload }
            );
            entering = true;
            form.reset();
            if (status)
                status.textContent = `Acesso confirmado. Iniciando a experiência URBS${result.user?.name ? ` para ${result.user.name}` : ''}.`;
        } catch (apiError) {
            error.textContent = apiError.message || 'Não foi possível entrar. Tente novamente.';
            if (button) {
                button.disabled = false;
                button.textContent = originalLabel;
            }
            document.querySelectorAll('.switch-link').forEach(item => item.disabled = false);
            return;
        }
        root.classList.add('is-departing');
        document.querySelectorAll('.submit-button,.switch-link').forEach(btn => btn.disabled = true);
        const beginFlight = () => {
            if (window.URBSGeoDescent)
                window.URBSGeoDescent.start();
            else {
                const overlay = document.querySelector('.arrival-overlay');
                overlay.style.display = 'flex';
                overlay.style.opacity = '1';
                setTimeout(() => location.assign('index.html'), 1500);
            }
        };
        // Primeiro desaparecem formulário e cabeçalho; só então aparece o Cesium.
        const elements = [...document.querySelectorAll('.form-side:not([aria-hidden="true"]),.topline,.bottomline')];
        if (gsap && !reduced) {
            gsap.to(elements, { autoAlpha: 0, y: -20, duration: .55, stagger: .035, ease: 'power2.inOut', onComplete: beginFlight });
        }
        else {
            elements.forEach(el => el.style.visibility = 'hidden');
            beginFlight();
        }
    }
    document.getElementById('login-form')?.addEventListener('submit', enterExperience);
    document.getElementById('register-form')?.addEventListener('submit', enterExperience);
    initGlobe();
})();


===== ARQUIVO: public\assets\js\digital-twin-fallback.js =====
// A CDN pode estar bloqueada em redes escolares. Mostre um aviso claro sem travar a página.
document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', () => {
    const stage = document.getElementById('city-stage');
    if (!stage)
        return;
    window.setTimeout(() => {
        if (stage.dataset.twinState === 'ready' || stage.dataset.twinState === 'error')
            return;
        const box = document.getElementById('twin-loading');
        if (box) {
            box.querySelector('strong').textContent = 'RECURSO 3D NÃO CARREGOU';
            box.querySelector('small').textContent = 'Verifique o acesso ao Three.js e o suporte WebGL. O mapa e o restante do site continuam disponíveis.';
            box.querySelector('.twin-loading-orbit')?.remove();
        }
    }, 10000);
});

/** URBS — configuração opcional de imagens/terreno 3D.
 * Mantendo as chaves vazias, usa CesiumJS + imagem de satélite Esri, com fallback de ruas.
 * Uma API key usada no navegador é pública: RESTRINJA por domínio e API no provedor.
 * Nunca coloque credenciais secretas ou tokens de servidor neste arquivo.
 * Google Photorealistic requer faturamento habilitado e cumprimento das atribuições.
 */
window.URBS_GEO_CONFIG = Object.freeze({
    googleMapsApiKey: '', // Opcional: Map Tiles API / Photorealistic 3D Tiles
    cesiumIonToken: '', // Opcional: Cesium World Terrain
    flightDurationSeconds: 6, // V8: duração do movimento de câmera, em segundos

===== ARQUIVO: public\assets\js\geo-descent.js =====
/* URBS — aproximação geográfica de Montes Claros com CesiumJS e GSAP. */
(() => {
  'use strict';

  const C = window.Cesium;
  const gsap = window.gsap;
  const config = window.URBS_GEO_CONFIG || {};
  const destination = config.destination || { longitude: -43.8578, latitude: -16.7286, label: 'Montes Claros, MG' };
  const root = document.getElementById('geo-flight');
  const sceneHost = document.getElementById('cesium-flight');
  const details = root.querySelector('.geo-details');
  const phase = document.getElementById('geo-phase');
  const place = document.getElementById('geo-place');
  const progress = document.getElementById('geo-progress-bar');
  const status = document.querySelector('.screen-reader-status');
  const route = [
    { t: 0, lon: -24, lat: 0, height: 18800000 },
    { t: .27, lon: -46, lat: -13, height: 8600000 },
    { t: .50, lon: -50, lat: -15, height: 3200000 },
    { t: .72, lon: -45.4, lat: -17.9, height: 520000 },
    { t: .89, lon: -43.96, lat: -16.92, height: 61000 },
    { t: 1, lon: destination.longitude, lat: destination.latitude, height: 10500 }
  ];
  const chapters = [
    { at: .14, name: 'América do Sul' },
    { at: .37, name: 'Brasil' },
    { at: .64, name: 'Minas Gerais' },
    { at: .88, name: destination.label.split(',')[0], caption: 'ÁREA PILOTO · MINAS GERAIS' }
  ];
  const wait = ms => new Promise(resolve => window.setTimeout(resolve, ms));

  let viewer;
  let running = false;
  let ended = false;
  let journey;
  let caption;
  let stopWaitingForTiles;
  let hasSatellite = false;
  let hasPhotorealisticTiles = false;

  function sample(values, u) {
    let index = 0;
    while (index < route.length - 2 && u > route[index + 1].t) {
      index++;
    }

    const duration = route[index + 1].t - route[index].t;
    const q = (u - route[index].t) / duration;
    const slope = i => (values[i + 1] - values[i]) / (route[i + 1].t - route[i].t);
    const tangent = i => {
      if (i === 0) return slope(0);
      if (i === route.length - 1) return slope(i - 1);
      const before = slope(i - 1);
      const after = slope(i);
      return before * after > 0 ? 2 * before * after / (before + after) : 0;
    };

    return (2 * q ** 3 - 3 * q ** 2 + 1) * values[index]
      + (q ** 3 - 2 * q ** 2 + q) * duration * tangent(index)
      + (-2 * q ** 3 + 3 * q ** 2) * values[index + 1]
      + (q ** 3 - q ** 2) * duration * tangent(index + 1);
  }

  const longitudes = route.map(point => point.lon);
  const latitudes = route.map(point => point.lat);
  const logHeights = route.map(point => Math.log(point.height));

  function positionCamera(u) {
    const tilt = Math.max(0, (u - .76) / .24);
    viewer.camera.setView({
      destination: C.Cartesian3.fromDegrees(
        sample(longitudes, u),
        sample(latitudes, u),
        Math.exp(sample(logHeights, u))
      ),
      orientation: { heading: 0, pitch: -C.Math.PI_OVER_TWO + .13 * tilt ** 2, roll: 0 }
    });
  }

  function finish() {
    if (ended) return;
    ended = true;
    try {
      sessionStorage.setItem('urbs-home-arrival-v9', String(Date.now()));
    } catch (_) { }
    window.location.assign('index.html');
  }

  function setCaption(name, label) {
    place.textContent = name;
    phase.textContent = label;
  }

  function changeChapter(chapter) {
    caption?.kill();
    caption = gsap.timeline()
      .to(details, { opacity: 0, y: -5, duration: .13, ease: 'power1.in' })
      .call(() => setCaption(chapter.name, chapter.caption || 'DO ESPAÇO À CIDADE'))
      .set(details, { y: 8 })
      .to(details, { opacity: 1, y: 0, duration: .31, ease: 'power2.out' });
  }

  async function prepareImagery() {
    const local = C.SingleTileImageryProvider.fromUrl('assets/textures/earth-day.jpg', {
      credit: 'NASA / Earth Observatory · Blue Marble'
    })
      .then(provider => {
        if (ended || hasPhotorealisticTiles) return false;
        viewer.imageryLayers.addImageryProvider(provider, 0);
        return true;
      })
      .catch(() => false);

    const satellite = C.ArcGisMapServerImageryProvider.fromUrl(
      'https://services.arcgisonline.com/ArcGIS/rest/services/World_Imagery/MapServer',
      { enablePickFeatures: false }
    ).then(provider => {
      if (ended || hasPhotorealisticTiles) return false;
      viewer.imageryLayers.addImageryProvider(provider);
      hasSatellite = true;
      return true;
    }).catch(async () => {
      if (await local) return false;
      if (ended || hasPhotorealisticTiles) return false;
      const provider = new C.OpenStreetMapImageryProvider({ url: 'https://tile.openstreetmap.org/' });
      viewer.imageryLayers.addImageryProvider(provider);
      return true;
    }).catch(() => false);

    // Prepara dados opcionais em paralelo com as imagens da superfície.
    if (config.cesiumIonToken) {
      C.Ion.defaultAccessToken = config.cesiumIonToken;
      C.createWorldTerrainAsync().then(terrain => {
        if (!ended) viewer.terrainProvider = terrain;
      }).catch(() => {});
    }

    if (config.googleMapsApiKey) {
      const tileUrl = 'https://tile.googleapis.com/v1/3dtiles/root.json?key=' + encodeURIComponent(config.googleMapsApiKey);
      C.Cesium3DTileset.fromUrl(tileUrl, { showCreditsOnScreen: true, maximumScreenSpaceError: 16 })
        .then(tileset => {
          if (ended) {
            tileset.destroy();
            return;
          }
          viewer.scene.primitives.add(tileset);
          viewer.scene.globe.show = false;
          hasPhotorealisticTiles = true;
        }).catch(() => {});
    }

    await Promise.race([satellite, wait(1000)]);
    if (ended || hasSatellite || hasPhotorealisticTiles) return;
    if (!await Promise.race([local, wait(500)]) && viewer.imageryLayers.length === 0) {
      throw new Error('Imagem geográfica indisponível.');
    }
  }

  function waitForInitialTiles() {
    return new Promise(resolve => {
      let renderedFrames = 0;
      function done() {
        window.clearTimeout(timeout);
        removeListener();
        stopWaitingForTiles = undefined;
        resolve();
      }
      const removeListener = viewer.scene.postRender.addEventListener(() => {
        renderedFrames++;
        if (renderedFrames >= 2 && (viewer.scene.globe.tilesLoaded || hasPhotorealisticTiles)) {
          done();
        }
      });
      const timeout = window.setTimeout(done, 850);
      stopWaitingForTiles = done;
    });
  }

  function runJourney() {
    const duration = Math.max(4, Number(config.flightDurationSeconds) || 6);
    const customEase = window.CustomEase;
    let cameraEase = 'sine.inOut';
    if (customEase) {
      gsap.registerPlugin(customEase);
      cameraEase = customEase.create(
        'urbs-orbital',
        'M0,0 C.09,0 .18,.09 .28,.27 C.37,.432 .42,.555 .51,.62 C.61,.692 .67,.728 .76,.795 C.87,.877 .90,1 1,1'
      );
    }
    const camera = { position: 0 };
    let nextChapter = 0;
    journey = gsap.timeline({ onComplete: finish });
    journey
      .to(sceneHost, { opacity: 1, duration: .7, ease: 'power2.out' })
      .to(camera, {
        position: 1,
        duration,
        ease: cameraEase,
        onUpdate() {
          positionCamera(camera.position);
          let chapter;
          while (nextChapter < chapters.length && camera.position >= chapters[nextChapter].at) {
            chapter = chapters[nextChapter++];
          }
          if (chapter) changeChapter(chapter);
        }
      }, .15)
      .to(progress, { scaleX: 1, duration, ease: 'none' }, .15)
      .call(() => {
        status.textContent = 'Chegada a ' + destination.label + '.';
      })
      .addLabel('arrival', '+=.28')
      .to(root, { '--geo-dissolve': 1, duration: .42, ease: 'sine.in' }, 'arrival');
  }

  async function start() {
    if (running) return;
    running = true;
    const reduced = window.matchMedia('(prefers-reduced-motion: reduce)').matches;
    if (reduced) {
      finish();
      return;
    }

    root.classList.add('geo-active');
    root.setAttribute('aria-hidden', 'false');
    document.querySelectorAll('.form-side').forEach(form => { form.inert = true; });
    status.textContent = 'Aproximação geográfica de ' + destination.label + '.';

    try {
      if (!C || !gsap) throw new Error('Bibliotecas da abertura indisponíveis.');
      const touchDevice = window.matchMedia('(pointer: coarse)').matches;
      viewer = new C.Viewer(sceneHost, {
        animation: false, timeline: false, fullscreenButton: false, homeButton: false,
        navigationHelpButton: false, sceneModePicker: false, baseLayerPicker: false,
        geocoder: false, infoBox: false, selectionIndicator: false, baseLayer: false,
        terrainProvider: new C.EllipsoidTerrainProvider(),
        useBrowserRecommendedResolution: false,
        msaaSamples: touchDevice ? 2 : 4
      });
      const framePixels = window.innerWidth * window.innerHeight;
      const ratio = window.devicePixelRatio || 1;
      const pixelBudget = touchDevice ? 2800000 : 4500000;
      const maxRatio = touchDevice ? 1.5 : 1.8;
      viewer.resolutionScale = Math.min(1, maxRatio / ratio, Math.sqrt(pixelBudget / framePixels) / ratio);
      viewer.scene.globe.enableLighting = false;
      viewer.scene.globe.maximumScreenSpaceError = touchDevice ? 1.8 : 1.45;
      viewer.scene.screenSpaceCameraController.enableInputs = false;
      positionCamera(0);
      await prepareImagery();
      if (ended) return;
      await waitForInitialTiles();
      if (!ended) runJourney();
    } catch (error) {
      if (ended) return;
      console.warn('URBS: abertura geográfica indisponível:', error);
      setCaption(destination.label.split(',')[0], 'ÁREA PILOTO · MINAS GERAIS');
      await wait(650);
      finish();
    }
  }

  window.URBSGeoDescent = { start };
  window.addEventListener('pagehide', () => {
    if (!running) return;
    ended = true;
    stopWaitingForTiles?.();
    journey?.kill();
    caption?.kill();
    viewer?.destroy();
  });
  window.addEventListener('pageshow', event => {
    if (event.persisted && running) window.location.reload();
  });
})();


===== ARQUIVO: public\assets\js\home-editorial.js =====
/* URBS — V9: transição de entrada e refinamentos de conteúdo. */
(() => {
    'use strict';
    const root = document.documentElement;
    if (root.classList.contains('urbs-home-arriving')) {
        const reveal = () => {
            root.classList.add('urbs-home-revealed');
            setTimeout(() => root.classList.remove('urbs-home-arriving', 'urbs-home-revealed'), 1200);
        };
        if (document.readyState === 'complete')
            setTimeout(reveal, 450);
        else
            window.addEventListener('load', () => setTimeout(reveal, 450), { once: true });
        setTimeout(reveal, 2300); // rede lenta nunca bloqueia a navegação
    }
    // Do not animate whole sections from opacity: 0. A failed trigger must never
    // hide the manifesto, territory information, or FAQ.
})();


===== ARQUIVO: public\assets\js\journey-gallery-v12-4.js =====
/* URBS V12.6 — seamless horizontal editorial reel + WebGL liquid distortion hover.
   Keeps the same horizontal carousel language already used in the site. */
(() => {
    'use strict';
    const viewport = document.getElementById('journey-reel');
    const track = viewport?.querySelector('.journey-reel-track');
    if (!viewport || !track)
        return;
    const originals = [...track.querySelectorAll('.journey-reel-card')];
    if (originals.length !== 3)
        return;
    const reduce = matchMedia('(prefers-reduced-motion: reduce)').matches;
    const speed = 43;
    originals.forEach(card => {
        const clone = card.cloneNode(true);
        clone.setAttribute('aria-hidden', 'true');
        clone.removeAttribute('tabindex');
        clone.inert = true;
        track.appendChild(clone);
    });
    let period = 0, offset = 0, oldTime = 0, raf = 0, isVisible = true, dragging = false;
    let pauseUntil = 0, dragOrigin = 0, dragOffset = 0, dragMoved = false;
    const wrap = n => period ? ((n % period) + period) % period : 0;
    const measure = () => {
        const duplicate = track.children[originals.length];
        period = duplicate.offsetLeft - originals[0].offsetLeft;
        offset = wrap(offset);
        viewport.scrollLeft = offset;
    };
    const tick = time => {
        raf = requestAnimationFrame(tick);
        const dt = oldTime ? Math.min(.05, (time - oldTime) / 1000) : 0;
        oldTime = time;
        if (reduce || !isVisible || document.hidden || dragging || time < pauseUntil || viewport.matches(':hover') || viewport.matches(':focus-within'))
            return;
        if (period) {
            offset = wrap(offset + dt * speed);
            viewport.scrollLeft = offset;
        }
    };
    viewport.addEventListener('pointerdown', e => {
        if (e.pointerType !== 'mouse' || e.button !== 0)
            return;
        dragging = true;
        dragMoved = false;
        dragOrigin = e.clientX;
        dragOffset = offset;
    });
    viewport.addEventListener('pointermove', e => {
        if (!dragging || !period)
            return;
        const dx = e.clientX - dragOrigin;
        if (Math.abs(dx) > 5)
            dragMoved = true;
        if (dragMoved) {
            offset = wrap(dragOffset - dx);
            viewport.scrollLeft = offset;
        }
    });
    const endDrag = () => { if (!dragging)
        return; dragging = false; pauseUntil = performance.now() + 1000; oldTime = 0; };
    window.addEventListener('pointerup', endDrag);
    window.addEventListener('pointercancel', endDrag);
    viewport.addEventListener('click', e => { if (dragMoved) {
        e.preventDefault();
        e.stopPropagation();
        dragMoved = false;
    } }, true);
    viewport.addEventListener('keydown', e => {
        if (e.key === 'ArrowLeft' || e.key === 'ArrowRight') {
            e.preventDefault();
            offset = wrap(offset + (e.key === 'ArrowRight' ? 1 : -1) * (originals[0].offsetWidth + 22));
            viewport.scrollLeft = offset;
            pauseUntil = performance.now() + 1800;
        }
    });
    const observer = ('IntersectionObserver' in window) ? new IntersectionObserver(entries => { isVisible = entries[0].isIntersecting; oldTime = 0; }, { threshold: .01 }) : null;
    observer?.observe(viewport);
    const resizer = ('ResizeObserver' in window) ? new ResizeObserver(measure) : null;
    resizer?.observe(viewport);
    resizer?.observe(track);
    window.addEventListener('resize', measure, { passive: true });
    measure();
    raf = requestAnimationFrame(tick);
    window.addEventListener('pagehide', () => { cancelAnimationFrame(raf); observer?.disconnect(); resizer?.disconnect(); }, { once: true });
    // V12.7 — Elastic liquid / wave displacement, localized beneath the cursor.
    // Keep images visible as CSS backgrounds until a WebGL canvas has loaded correctly.
    // Pixi is optional: broken CDN, blocked image CORS, reduced motion or unavailable WebGL
    // must not cause an empty carousel.
    if (reduce || !window.PIXI || !matchMedia('(hover:hover) and (pointer:fine)').matches)
        return;
    const fragment = `
 precision mediump float;
 varying vec2 vTextureCoord;
 uniform sampler2D uSampler;
 uniform vec2 uMouse;
 uniform vec2 uPrevious;
 uniform vec2 uVelocity;
 uniform float uStrength;
 uniform float uTime;
 uniform float uRatio;

 void main() {
   vec2 uv = vTextureCoord;
   vec2 ratio = vec2(uRatio, 1.0);
   vec2 delta = (uv - uMouse) * ratio;
   float distanceToMouse = length(delta);
   vec2 previousDelta = (uv - uPrevious) * ratio;
   float distanceToPrev = length(previousDelta);
   // Gaussian local brush + a broad soft wake pulled by the mouse velocity.
   float core = exp(-distanceToMouse * distanceToMouse * 32.0);
   float wake = exp(-distanceToPrev * distanceToPrev * 16.0) * 0.45;
   float rings = sin(distanceToMouse * 44.0 - uTime * 8.5);
   float slowWave = sin(distanceToMouse * 18.0 - uTime * 5.0);
   vec2 normal = normalize(delta + vec2(0.0001));
   vec2 movement = clamp(uVelocity, vec2(-0.25), vec2(0.25));
   vec2 directional = movement * (core * 0.32 + wake * 0.24);
   vec2 wave = normal * (rings * 0.009 + slowWave * 0.005) * (core + wake);
   // Fine gel-like refraction and light chromatic separation at the moving edge.
   vec2 offset = (directional + wave) * uStrength;
   vec2 at = clamp(uv - offset, 0.001, 0.999);
   float separation = 0.0016 * core * uStrength;
   vec2 chromatic = normal * separation;
   vec4 original = texture2D(uSampler, at);
   float r = texture2D(uSampler, clamp(at + chromatic, 0.001, 0.999)).r;
   float b = texture2D(uSampler, clamp(at - chromatic, 0.001, 0.999)).b;
   vec3 color = mix(original.rgb, vec3(r, original.g, b), 0.52 * uStrength);
   color += 0.037 * core * uStrength;
   gl_FragColor = vec4(color, original.a);
 }
 `;
    const activeInstances = new Set();
    let activeCount = 0;
    const addLiquid = card => {
        // Each instance is created only when hovered, saving GPU memory on initial page load.
        const media = card.querySelector('.journey-reel-media');
        const url = media?.dataset.image;
        if (!media || !url)
            return;
        let instance = null, loading = false, mounted = false;
        let wantsHover = false;
        const load = () => {
            if (instance || loading || !window.PIXI)
                return;
            loading = true;
            const image = new Image();
            image.crossOrigin = 'anonymous';
            image.decoding = 'async';
            image.onload = () => {
                try {
                    const app = new PIXI.Application({
                        resizeTo: media,
                        backgroundAlpha: 0,
                        antialias: true,
                        resolution: Math.min(devicePixelRatio || 1, 1.5),
                        autoDensity: true,
                        autoStart: false,
                        powerPreference: 'high-performance'
                    });
                    const texture = PIXI.Texture.from(image);
                    const sprite = new PIXI.Sprite(texture);
                    const filter = new PIXI.Filter(undefined, fragment, {
                        uMouse: new Float32Array([.5, .5]),
                        uPrevious: new Float32Array([.5, .5]),
                        uVelocity: new Float32Array([0, 0]),
                        uStrength: 0,
                        uTime: 0,
                        uRatio: 1
                    });
                    sprite.filters = [filter];
                    app.stage.addChild(sprite);
                    const state = { app, sprite, filter, mouse: [.5, .5], smooth: [.5, .5], last: [.5, .5],
                        strength: 0, target: 0, velocity: [0, 0], time: 0, hovered: false,
                        width: 0, height: 0, observer: null };
                    const fit = () => {
                        const w = media.clientWidth, h = media.clientHeight;
                        if (!w || !h)
                            return;
                        const iw = texture.width || 1, ih = texture.height || 1;
                        const scale = Math.max(w / iw, h / ih);
                        sprite.width = iw * scale;
                        sprite.height = ih * scale;
                        sprite.x = (w - sprite.width) / 2;
                        sprite.y = (h - sprite.height) / 2;
                        filter.uniforms.uRatio = sprite.width / sprite.height;
                        state.width = w;
                        state.height = h;
                        // Keep WebGL canvas precisely aligned with the CSS background.
                        app.renderer.resize(w, h);
                        app.render();
                    };
                    state.observer = new ResizeObserver(fit);
                    state.observer.observe(media);
                    instance = state;
                    activeInstances.add(state);
                    fit();
                    // The original CSS image remains underneath if WebGL cannot render.
                    media.appendChild(app.view);
                    media.classList.add('liquid-ready');
                    media.dataset.liquidReady = '1';
                    const update = delta => {
                        if (document.hidden || !card.isConnected)
                            return;
                        state.time += Math.min(delta, 3) / 60;
                        const follow = state.hovered ? 0.18 : 0.11;
                        state.smooth[0] += (state.mouse[0] - state.smooth[0]) * follow;
                        state.smooth[1] += (state.mouse[1] - state.smooth[1]) * follow;
                        const vx = state.smooth[0] - state.last[0];
                        const vy = state.smooth[1] - state.last[1];
                        state.velocity[0] += (vx - state.velocity[0]) * .22;
                        state.velocity[1] += (vy - state.velocity[1]) * .22;
                        state.last[0] = state.smooth[0];
                        state.last[1] = state.smooth[1];
                        state.strength += (state.target - state.strength) * 0.09;
                        const u = filter.uniforms;
                        u.uMouse = state.smooth;
                        u.uPrevious = [state.smooth[0] - state.velocity[0] * 9, state.smooth[1] - state.velocity[1] * 9];
                        u.uVelocity = state.velocity;
                        u.uStrength = state.strength;
                        u.uTime = state.time;
                        if (!state.hovered && state.strength < .007) {
                            state.strength = 0;
                            u.uStrength = 0;
                            app.ticker.stop();
                            app.render();
                        }
                    };
                    app.ticker.add(update);
                    if (wantsHover)
                        enter();
                    else
                        app.render();
                }
                catch (error) {
                    console.warn('[URBS liquid] WebGL unavailable; keeping original image.', error);
                    loading = false;
                }
            };
            image.onerror = () => { loading = false; console.warn('[URBS liquid] Image could not be loaded for WebGL; CSS image kept.'); };
            image.src = url;
        };
        const position = (e) => {
            if (!instance)
                return;
            const r = media.getBoundingClientRect();
            const x = Math.max(0, Math.min(r.width, e.clientX - r.left));
            const y = Math.max(0, Math.min(r.height, e.clientY - r.top));
            const sprite = instance.sprite;
            instance.mouse[0] = Math.max(0, Math.min(1, (x - sprite.x) / sprite.width));
            instance.mouse[1] = Math.max(0, Math.min(1, (y - sprite.y) / sprite.height));
        };
        const enter = () => {
            if (!instance)
                return;
            instance.hovered = true;
            instance.target = 1.15;
            instance.app.ticker.start();
        };
        card.addEventListener('pointerenter', e => { wantsHover = true; load(); if (instance) {
            position(e);
            enter();
        } });
        card.addEventListener('pointermove', e => { if (instance && !dragging)
            position(e); }, { passive: true });
        card.addEventListener('pointerleave', () => {
            wantsHover = false;
            if (instance) {
                instance.hovered = false;
                instance.target = 0;
                instance.app.ticker.start();
            }
        });
    };
    track.querySelectorAll('.journey-reel-card').forEach(addLiquid);
    window.addEventListener('pagehide', () => {
        for (const state of activeInstances) {
            state.observer?.disconnect();
            state.app.destroy(true, { children: true, texture: false, baseTexture: false });
        }
        activeInstances.clear();
    }, { once: true });
})();


===== ARQUIVO: public\assets\js\journey-v12-3.js =====
/* URBS V12.3 — rail feedback only, no scroll interception. */
(() => {
    'use strict';
    const root = document.querySelector('.journey-v123');
    if (!root)
        return;
    const cards = [...root.querySelectorAll('.journey-card')];
    const activate = (step) => {
        root.dataset.active = String(step);
        root.style.setProperty('--step-progress', step === 1 ? '0' : step === 2 ? '0.5' : '1');
    };
    cards.forEach((card, index) => {
        card.addEventListener('pointerenter', () => activate(index + 1));
        card.addEventListener('focusin', () => activate(index + 1));
    });
    root.querySelector('.journey-grid')?.addEventListener('pointerleave', () => {
        root.removeAttribute('data-active');
        root.style.setProperty('--step-progress', '0');
    });
    root.addEventListener('focusout', e => {
        if (!root.contains(e.relatedTarget)) {
            root.removeAttribute('data-active');
            root.style.setProperty('--step-progress', '0');
        }
    });
})();


===== ARQUIVO: public\assets\js\main.js =====
document.addEventListener("DOMContentLoaded", () => {
    const menuButton = document.querySelector(".menu-toggle");
    const nav = document.querySelector(".main-nav");
    const progress = document.querySelector(".scroll-progress span");
    function closeMenu() {
        if (!nav || !menuButton)
            return;
        nav.classList.remove("is-open");
        menuButton.setAttribute("aria-expanded", "false");
        menuButton.setAttribute("aria-label", "Abrir navegação");
    }
    menuButton?.addEventListener("click", () => {
        const open = nav?.classList.toggle("is-open") ?? false;
        menuButton.setAttribute("aria-expanded", String(open));
        menuButton.setAttribute("aria-label", open ? "Fechar navegação" : "Abrir navegação");
    });
    nav?.querySelectorAll("a").forEach((link) => link.addEventListener("click", closeMenu));
    document.addEventListener("keydown", (event) => {
        if (event.key === "Escape")
            closeMenu();
    });
    function updateProgress() {
        if (!progress)
            return;
        const max = document.documentElement.scrollHeight - window.innerHeight;
        progress.style.transform = `scaleX(${max > 0 ? window.scrollY / max : 0})`;
    }
    window.addEventListener("scroll", updateProgress, { passive: true });
    updateProgress();
    const count = document.getElementById("local-record-count");
    if (count && window.UrbsData)
        count.textContent = String(window.UrbsData.getStoredReports().length);
    // Scroll animations are intentionally non-blocking; all content stays visible.
});


===== ARQUIVO: public\assets\js\map-arrival.js =====
/* URBS V8 — chegada ao mapa. Sem autenticação, sem persistir dados pessoais. */
(() => {
    'use strict';
    if (!document.documentElement.classList.contains('urbs-is-arriving'))
        return;
    let released = false;
    function reveal() {
        if (released)
            return;
        released = true;
        document.documentElement.classList.add('urbs-arrival-reveal');
        // A transição da interface pode começar mesmo sem carregar as tiles.
        setTimeout(() => document.documentElement.classList.remove('urbs-is-arriving', 'urbs-arrival-reveal'), 1150);
    }
    // Não segurar o usuário à espera da rede. Máximo de 900 ms após DOM pronto.
    document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', () => {
        const map = document.getElementById('map');
        let started = false;
        const show = () => { if (started)
            return; started = true; setTimeout(reveal, 180); };
        if (map) {
            const observer = new MutationObserver(() => {
                if (map.querySelector('.leaflet-tile-loaded')) {
                    observer.disconnect();
                    show();
                }
            });
            observer.observe(map, { childList: true, subtree: true, attributes: true, attributeFilter: ['class'] });
            setTimeout(() => { observer.disconnect(); show(); }, 750);
        }
        else
            show();
    });
    setTimeout(reveal, 2000); // fail-safe para erro de conexão / arquivos.
})();


===== ARQUIVO: public\assets\js\map.js =====
document.addEventListener("DOMContentLoaded", () => {
    const mapElement = document.getElementById("map");
    const mapUnavailable = document.getElementById("map-unavailable");
    const data = window.UrbsData;
    if (!mapElement || !data)
        return;
    const center = [-16.7286, -43.8578];
    const categoryMarkers = new Map([
        ["Pavimentação", { icon: "construction", symbol: "║", color: "#f4a96e", examples: "Buracos, asfalto quebrado e desníveis na via." }],
        ["Iluminação pública", { icon: "lightbulb", symbol: "✦", color: "#f8df75", examples: "Lâmpadas apagadas, intermitentes ou danificadas." }],
        ["Acessibilidade", { icon: "accessibility", symbol: "♿︎", color: "#9ce0a3", examples: "Calçadas obstruídas, rampas danificadas ou ausentes." }],
        ["Descarte irregular de resíduos", { icon: "trash-2", symbol: "▤", color: "#c9b7f4", examples: "Lixo, entulho ou móveis descartados em área pública." }],
        ["Drenagem", { icon: "droplets", symbol: "≋", color: "#8dcdf3", examples: "Bueiros entupidos e acúmulo de água na rua." }],
        ["Sinalização", { icon: "signpost", symbol: "!", color: "#f6a6bf", examples: "Placas danificadas, semáforos com defeito e faixas apagadas." }],
        ["Outro", { icon: "map-pin", symbol: "•", color: "#cbd4ca", examples: "Problemas de infraestrutura fora das demais categorias." }]
    ]);
    const otherCategory = categoryMarkers.get("Outro");
    const statusColors = {
        "Novo": "#b8e954",
        "Em análise": "#e5a94a",
        "Em andamento": "#53c4aa",
        "Solucionado": "#6dae71",
        "Rejeitado": "#d27a76"
    };
    const statusClass = {
        "Novo": "",
        "Em análise": "status-analysis",
        "Em andamento": "status-progress",
        "Solucionado": "status-solved",
        "Rejeitado": "status-rejected"
    };
    const statusFilter = document.getElementById("status-filter");
    const categoryFilter = document.getElementById("category-filter");
    const locationFilter = document.getElementById("location-filter");
    const list = document.getElementById("report-list");
    const emptyState = document.getElementById("empty-state");
    const resultCount = document.getElementById("result-count");
    const activeFilterCount = document.getElementById("active-filter-count");
    const clearButton = document.getElementById("clear-filters");
    const locateButton = document.getElementById("locate-me");
    const liveRegion = document.getElementById("map-live-region");
    const actionFeedback = document.getElementById("report-action-feedback");
    const explorer = document.getElementById("occurrence-explorer");
    const explorerToggle = document.getElementById("explorer-toggle");
    const isMobile = window.matchMedia("(max-width: 760px)");
    if (explorer && explorerToggle) {
        explorer.dataset.expanded = "false";
        explorerToggle.addEventListener("click", () => {
            const expanded = explorer.dataset.expanded === "false";
            explorer.dataset.expanded = String(expanded);
            explorerToggle.setAttribute("aria-expanded", String(expanded));
        });
    }
    renderPinGuide();
    if (!window.L) {
        if (mapUnavailable)
            mapUnavailable.hidden = false;
        return;
    }
    // O mapa mantém os exemplos visuais e carrega as ocorrências reais pela API PHP.
    const map = L.map(mapElement, { scrollWheelZoom: false, zoomControl: false, preferCanvas: true }).setView(center, 13);
    const streets = L.tileLayer('https://tile.openstreetmap.org/{z}/{x}/{y}.png', {
        maxZoom: 19, attribution: '&copy; <a href="https://www.openstreetmap.org/copyright">OpenStreetMap</a> contributors'
    });
    const satellite = L.tileLayer('https://server.arcgisonline.com/ArcGIS/rest/services/World_Imagery/MapServer/tile/{z}/{y}/{x}', {
        maxZoom: 19, attribution: 'Imagens: Esri, Maxar, Earthstar Geographics e colaboradores'
    });
    const labels = L.tileLayer('https://server.arcgisonline.com/ArcGIS/rest/services/Reference/World_Boundaries_and_Places/MapServer/tile/{z}/{y}/{x}', {
        maxZoom: 19, opacity: .88, attribution: 'Rótulos: Esri'
    });
    let selectedLayer = 'light';
    streets.addTo(map);
    L.control.zoom({ position: 'bottomright' }).addTo(map);
    const lightButton = document.getElementById('btnLayerLight');
    const satButton = document.getElementById('btnLayerSat');
    function setMapLayer(mode) {
        if (mode !== 'light' && mode !== 'satellite')
            return;
        selectedLayer = mode;
        map.removeLayer(streets);
        map.removeLayer(satellite);
        map.removeLayer(labels);
        if (mode === 'satellite') {
            satellite.addTo(map);
            labels.addTo(map);
        }
        else {
            streets.addTo(map);
        }
        lightButton?.classList.toggle('is-active', mode === 'light');
        satButton?.classList.toggle('is-active', mode === 'satellite');
        lightButton?.setAttribute('aria-pressed', String(mode === 'light'));
        satButton?.setAttribute('aria-pressed', String(mode === 'satellite'));
        if (liveRegion)
            liveRegion.textContent = mode === 'light' ? 'Mapa de ruas selecionado.' : 'Visualização por satélite selecionada.';
    }
    lightButton?.addEventListener('click', () => setMapLayer('light'));
    satButton?.addEventListener('click', () => setMapLayer('satellite'));
    let failedSatelliteTiles = 0;
    satellite.on('tileerror', () => {
        if (selectedLayer === 'satellite' && ++failedSatelliteTiles >= 5) {
            setMapLayer('light');
            if (liveRegion)
                liveRegion.textContent = 'Imagens de satélite indisponíveis. Alternamos para o mapa de ruas.';
        }
    });
    const markerLayer = L.featureGroup().addTo(map);
    const markersById = new Map();
    const resetCityButton = document.getElementById('map-reset-city');
    const fitReportsButton = document.getElementById('map-fit-reports');
    function centerCity() { map.flyTo(center, 15, { duration: 1.25 }); }
    function showAllPoints() {
        if (markerLayer.getLayers().length && markerLayer.getBounds().isValid()) {
            map.flyToBounds(markerLayer.getBounds().pad(.22), { maxZoom: 14, duration: 1.35 });
        }
        else {
            centerCity();
        }
    }
    resetCityButton?.addEventListener('click', centerCity);
    fitReportsButton?.addEventListener('click', showAllPoints);
    function normalizeText(value) {
        return String(value || "").normalize("NFD").replace(/[\u0300-\u036f]/g, "").toLocaleLowerCase("pt-BR");
    }
    function makeElement(tag, className, text) {
        const element = document.createElement(tag);
        if (className)
            element.className = className;
        if (text !== undefined)
            element.textContent = text;
        return element;
    }
    function getFilteredReports() {
        const category = categoryFilter?.value || "all";
        const status = statusFilter?.value || "all";
        const term = normalizeText(locationFilter?.value || "").trim();
        return data.getAllReports().filter((report) => {
            const matchesCategory = category === "all" || report.category === category;
            const matchesStatus = status === "all" || report.status === status;
            const haystack = normalizeText(`${report.address} ${report.title} ${report.description}`);
            return matchesCategory && matchesStatus && (!term || haystack.includes(term));
        }).sort((a, b) => Date.parse(b.createdAt) - Date.parse(a.createdAt));
    }
    function popupContent(report) {
        const wrapper = makeElement("div", "map-popup");
        wrapper.append(makeElement("small", "", report.category));
        wrapper.append(makeElement("strong", "", report.title));
        wrapper.append(makeElement("p", "", report.description));
        wrapper.append(makeElement("span", "popup-meta", `${report.address || "Localização marcada no mapa"} · ${data.formatDate(report.createdAt)} · ${report.status}`));
        if (report.demo)
            wrapper.append(makeElement("span", "demo-badge", "EXEMPLO FICTÍCIO"));
        else if (data.canEdit(report))
            wrapper.append(makeReportActions(report));
        return wrapper;
    }
    function showActionFeedback(message, error = false) {
        actionFeedback.classList.toggle("is-error", error);
        actionFeedback.textContent = message;
    }
    function makeReportActions(report) {
        const actions = makeElement("div", "report-management");
        const remove = makeElement("button", "report-action report-action-delete", "Excluir");
        if (data.canEdit(report)) {
            const edit = makeElement("a", "report-action", "Editar");
            edit.href = `registro.html?${new URLSearchParams({ editar: report.id })}`;
            edit.setAttribute("aria-label", `Editar ocorrência: ${report.title}`);
            actions.append(edit);
        }
        remove.type = "button";
        remove.setAttribute("aria-label", `Excluir ocorrência: ${report.title}`);
        remove.addEventListener("click", async () => {
            if (!window.confirm(`Excluir “${report.title}”? Esta ação não pode ser desfeita.`))
                return;
            remove.disabled = true;
            const result = await data.deleteReport(report.id);
            if (!result.ok) {
                showActionFeedback(result.message || "Não foi possível excluir a ocorrência.", true);
                remove.disabled = false;
                return;
            }
            render();
            showActionFeedback("Ocorrência excluída do banco de dados.");
            const focusTarget = isMobile.matches && explorer?.dataset.expanded === "false" ? explorerToggle : locationFilter;
            focusTarget?.focus({ preventScroll: true });
        });
        if (data.currentUser?.role === "master" || report.userId === Number(data.currentUser?.id))
            actions.append(remove);
        return actions;
    }
    function makeStatusSelect(report) {
        const select = document.createElement("select");
        select.className = "status-select";
        select.dataset.statusFor = report.id;
        select.setAttribute("aria-label", `Atualizar situação de ${report.title}`);
        data.STATUSES.forEach((status) => {
            const option = document.createElement("option");
            option.value = status;
            option.textContent = status;
            option.selected = status === report.status;
            select.append(option);
        });
        return select;
    }
    function makeReportCard(report) {
        const card = makeElement("article", "report-card");
        card.dataset.reportId = report.id;
        const topline = makeElement("div", "report-card-topline");
        const category = makeElement("span", "report-card-category");
        const appearance = categoryMarkers.get(report.category) || otherCategory;
        const symbol = makeElement("span", "report-card-symbol", appearance.symbol);
        symbol.style.setProperty("background-color", appearance.color);
        symbol.setAttribute("aria-hidden", "true");
        category.append(symbol, makeElement("span", "", report.category));
        card.append(category);
        if (report.demo)
            topline.append(makeElement("span", "demo-badge", "EXEMPLO"));
        const badge = makeElement("span", `status-pill ${statusClass[report.status] || ""}`, report.status);
        topline.append(badge);
        card.append(topline);
        const titleLine = makeElement("h3");
        const focusButton = makeElement("button", "report-card-title", report.title);
        focusButton.type = "button";
        focusButton.dataset.focusId = report.id;
        focusButton.setAttribute("aria-pressed", "false");
        focusButton.setAttribute("aria-label", `Ver no mapa: ${report.title}`);
        titleLine.append(focusButton);
        card.append(titleLine);
        const meta = makeElement("div", "report-card-meta");
        meta.textContent = report.address || "Ponto marcado no mapa";
        card.append(meta);
        if (!report.demo && data.canEdit(report)) {
            const actions = makeReportActions(report);
            card.append(actions);
        }
        if (!report.demo && data.canManageStatus())
            card.append(makeStatusSelect(report));
        return card;
    }
    function makePin(appearance, status, demo) {
        const pin = makeElement("span", demo ? "map-v19-pin map-v19-demo-pin" : "map-v19-pin");
        pin.setAttribute("aria-hidden", "true");
        pin.style.setProperty("--pin-color", appearance.color);
        const icon = makeElement("span", "map-pin-icon");
        const iconNode = makeElement("i", "map-pin-lucide");
        icon.append(iconNode);
        if (window.lucide?.createIcons) {
            iconNode.setAttribute("data-lucide", appearance.icon || "map-pin");
            window.lucide.createIcons({ root: icon, attrs: { "stroke-width": 2.2 } });
        } else {
            icon.classList.add("map-pin-icon-fallback");
            icon.textContent = appearance.symbol || "•";
        }
        pin.append(icon);
        if (status) {
            pin.style.setProperty("--pin-status", statusColors[status] || statusColors.Novo);
            pin.append(makeElement("span", "map-pin-status"));
        }
        return pin;
    }
    function renderPinGuide() {
        const guide = document.getElementById("map-pin-guide-list");
        if (!guide)
            return;
        const groupNames = {
            "Pavimentação": "MOBILIDADE URBANA",
            "Iluminação pública": "SERVIÇOS URBANOS",
            "Acessibilidade": "ACESSIBILIDADE",
            "Descarte irregular de resíduos": "LIMPEZA URBANA",
            "Drenagem": "INFRAESTRUTURA",
            "Sinalização": "MOBILIDADE URBANA",
            "Outro": "INFRAESTRUTURA URBANA"
        };
        categoryMarkers.forEach((appearance, category, categories) => {
            const card = makeElement("li", "map-pin-guide-card");
            const preview = makeElement("span", "map-pin-preview");
            const top = makeElement("div", "map-pin-guide-top");
            const index = String(guide.children.length + 1).padStart(2, "0");
            const title = makeElement("h3", "map-pin-guide-title");
            const categoryName = makeElement("span", "map-pin-guide-category", category);
            const exampleLabel = makeElement("span", "map-pin-guide-example", "— EXEMPLO");
            const description = makeElement("p", "map-pin-guide-description", appearance.examples);
            const meta = makeElement("div", "map-pin-guide-meta");
            const metaCopy = makeElement("div", "map-pin-guide-meta-copy");
            const metaLabel = makeElement("span", "", "COR DO MARCADOR");
            const metaValue = makeElement("strong", "", appearance.color.toUpperCase());
            const footer = makeElement("div", "map-pin-guide-footer");
            const footerText = makeElement("span", "", "O pino colorido identifica esta categoria no mapa.");
            preview.setAttribute("aria-hidden", "true");
            preview.append(makePin(appearance));
            card.style.setProperty("--guide-pin-color", appearance.color);
            top.append(makeElement("span", "map-pin-guide-index", `${index} / ${String(categories.size).padStart(2, "0")}`));
            top.append(makeElement("span", "map-pin-guide-group", groupNames[category] || "URBANISMO"));
            title.append(categoryName, exampleLabel);
            metaCopy.append(metaLabel, metaValue);
            meta.append(metaCopy);
            footer.append(footerText, preview);
            card.append(top, title, description, meta, footer);
            guide.append(card);
        });
        guide.closest("section").hidden = false;
    }
    function makeReportMarker(report) {
        const appearance = categoryMarkers.get(report.category) || otherCategory;
        const pin = makePin(appearance, report.status, report.demo);
        const icon = L.divIcon({
            className: "map-v19-pin-holder",
            html: pin.outerHTML,
            iconSize: [44, 48], iconAnchor: [22, 46], popupAnchor: [8, -45]
        });
        const label = `${report.title} · ${report.category} · ${report.status}`;
        const marker = L.marker([report.lat, report.lng], { icon, keyboard: true, title: label }).addTo(markerLayer);
        marker.getElement()?.setAttribute("aria-label", label);
        return marker;
    }
    function render() {
        const reports = getFilteredReports();
        markerLayer.clearLayers();
        markersById.clear();
        list.replaceChildren();
        reports.forEach((report) => {
            list.append(makeReportCard(report));
            const marker = makeReportMarker(report);
            marker.bindPopup(popupContent(report), { maxWidth: 260 });
            marker.on("popupopen", () => selectReport(report.id));
            markersById.set(report.id, marker);
        });
        resultCount.textContent = `${reports.length} ${reports.length === 1 ? "registro" : "registros"}`;
        const active = [categoryFilter, statusFilter].filter((field) => field?.value && field.value !== "all").length;
        activeFilterCount.hidden = active === 0;
        activeFilterCount.textContent = String(active);
        emptyState.hidden = reports.length > 0;
        if (reports.length > 0 && markerLayer.getBounds().isValid()) {
            map.fitBounds(markerLayer.getBounds().pad(.22), { maxZoom: 14, animate: false });
        }
        else {
            map.setView(center, 13);
        }
    }
    let selectedReportId = null;
    let pendingPopup = 0;
    function selectReport(id) {
        selectedReportId = id;
        list.querySelectorAll(".report-card").forEach((card) => {
            card.classList.toggle("is-selected", card.dataset.reportId === id);
            card.querySelector("[data-focus-id]").setAttribute("aria-pressed", String(card.dataset.reportId === id));
        });
        markersById.forEach((marker, markerId) => {
            const selected = markerId === id;
            marker.getElement()?.classList.toggle("is-selected", selected);
            marker.setZIndexOffset(selected ? 1000 : 0);
        });
    }
    function focusReport(id, animate = true) {
        const marker = markersById.get(id);
        if (!marker)
            return false;
        const position = marker.getLatLng();
        selectReport(id);
        window.clearTimeout(pendingPopup);
        if (animate) {
            map.flyTo(position, 16, { duration: 1.15 });
            pendingPopup = window.setTimeout(() => {
                if (markersById.get(id) === marker && selectedReportId === id)
                    marker.openPopup();
            }, 400);
        }
        else {
            map.setView(position, 16, { animate: false });
            marker.openPopup();
        }
        return true;
    }
    [categoryFilter, statusFilter].forEach((field) => field?.addEventListener("change", render));
    locationFilter?.addEventListener("input", render);
    clearButton?.addEventListener("click", () => {
        categoryFilter.value = "all";
        statusFilter.value = "all";
        locationFilter.value = "";
        render();
        locationFilter.focus();
    });
    list.addEventListener("click", (event) => {
        const button = event.target.closest("[data-focus-id]");
        if (button && focusReport(button.dataset.focusId, !isMobile.matches) && isMobile.matches) {
            mapElement.scrollIntoView({ block: "nearest", behavior: "instant" });
        }
    });
    list.addEventListener("change", async (event) => {
        const select = event.target.closest("[data-status-for]");
        if (!select)
            return;
        select.disabled = true;
        const updated = await data.updateStatus(select.dataset.statusFor, select.value);
        if (liveRegion)
            liveRegion.textContent = updated.ok
                ? `Situação atualizada para ${select.value}.`
                : updated.message || "Não foi possível atualizar a situação.";
        if (!updated.ok)
            showActionFeedback(updated.message || "Não foi possível atualizar a situação.", true);
        render();
    });
    locateButton?.addEventListener("click", () => {
        if (!navigator.geolocation) {
            if (liveRegion)
                liveRegion.textContent = "A geolocalização não está disponível neste dispositivo.";
            return;
        }
        locateButton.disabled = true;
        navigator.geolocation.getCurrentPosition((position) => {
            map.flyTo([position.coords.latitude, position.coords.longitude], 16, { duration: 1.25 });
            locateButton.disabled = false;
            if (liveRegion)
                liveRegion.textContent = "Mapa centralizado na sua localização.";
        }, () => {
            locateButton.disabled = false;
            if (liveRegion)
                liveRegion.textContent = "Não foi possível obter sua localização. Confira as permissões do dispositivo.";
        }, { enableHighAccuracy: true, timeout: 10000, maximumAge: 60000 });
    });
    function updateRegistrationLinks() {
        document.querySelectorAll('a[href="registro.html"]').forEach((link) => {
            if (data.currentUser) {
                link.href = "registro.html";
                if (link.classList.contains("occurrence-primary"))
                    link.innerHTML = 'Registrar <span aria-hidden="true">＋</span>';
            } else {
                link.href = "login.html";
                if (link.classList.contains("occurrence-primary"))
                    link.textContent = "Entrar para registrar";
                else
                    link.textContent = "Entrar para registrar ocorrência";
            }
        });
    }

    render();
    data.load().then(({ error }) => {
        render();
        updateRegistrationLinks();
        if (error)
            showActionFeedback(error.message, true);
        const params = new URLSearchParams(window.location.search);
        const editedReportId = params.get("editado");
        const requestedReportId = editedReportId || params.get("novo");
        if (requestedReportId && focusReport(requestedReportId, false)) {
            if (editedReportId)
                showActionFeedback("Alterações salvas. Ocorrência selecionada no mapa.");
            else if (liveRegion)
                liveRegion.textContent = "Registro salvo no banco de dados. Ocorrência selecionada no mapa.";
            mapElement.scrollIntoView({ block: "nearest", behavior: "instant" });
        }
    });
    window.addEventListener("pageshow", async (event) => {
        if (event.persisted) {
            await data.load();
            render();
            updateRegistrationLinks();
        }
    });
    const params = new URLSearchParams(window.location.search);
    window.setTimeout(() => map.invalidateSize(), 120);
});




===== ARQUIVO: public\assets\js\media-experience.js =====
/* URBS V10 - Each library owns its own section. Progressive enhancement, no fake external media. */
(() => {
    'use strict';
    const safe = (name, callback) => { try {
        callback();
    }
    catch (error) {
        console.warn('[URBS media] ' + name, error);
    } };
    const reduceMotion = matchMedia('(prefers-reduced-motion: reduce)').matches;
    const ready = () => {
        safe('Embla', () => {
            const root = document.querySelector('.urbs-stories-embla');
            if (!root || !window.EmblaCarousel)
                return;
            const api = EmblaCarousel(root.querySelector('.embla__viewport'), {
                align: 'start',
                containScroll: 'trimSnaps',
                loop: true,
                dragFree: false,
                duration: reduceMotion ? 0 : 32
            });
            const AUTO_DELAY = 3400;
            let autoTimer = null;
            const stopAuto = () => { if (autoTimer) {
                clearInterval(autoTimer);
                autoTimer = null;
            } };
            const startAuto = () => {
                stopAuto();
                if (reduceMotion)
                    return;
                autoTimer = setInterval(() => { api.scrollNext(); }, AUTO_DELAY);
            };
            const pauseThenResume = () => {
                stopAuto();
                window.clearTimeout(root._urbsResumeTimer);
                root._urbsResumeTimer = window.setTimeout(startAuto, 2200);
            };
            root.addEventListener('mouseenter', stopAuto);
            root.addEventListener('mouseleave', startAuto);
            root.addEventListener('focusin', stopAuto);
            root.addEventListener('focusout', startAuto);
            root.addEventListener('pointerdown', pauseThenResume);
            root.addEventListener('touchstart', pauseThenResume, { passive: true });
            api.on('pointerDown', stopAuto);
            api.on('settle', () => {
                if (!root.matches(':hover') && !root.matches(':focus-within'))
                    startAuto();
            });
            startAuto();
        });
        safe('Splide', () => {
            if (!window.Splide)
                return;
            new Splide('#urbs-highlights-splide', {
                type: 'slide', perPage: 3, perMove: 1, gap: '18px',
                pagination: false, arrows: true, drag: true, speed: reduceMotion ? 0 : 650,
                breakpoints: { 980: { perPage: 2 }, 660: { perPage: 1 } }
            }).mount();
        });
        safe('GLightbox', () => { if (window.GLightbox)
            GLightbox({ selector: '.glightbox', touchNavigation: true, loop: false }); });
        safe('Plyr', () => { if (window.Plyr)
            window.urbsPlyr = new Plyr('#urbs-plyr', { controls: ['play-large', 'play', 'progress', 'current-time', 'mute', 'volume', 'fullscreen'] }); });
        safe('player-switch', () => {
            const button = document.getElementById('change-player');
            if (!button)
                return;
            button.addEventListener('click', () => {
                let video = document.getElementById('urbs-plyr');
                if (!video)
                    return;
                const useVideoJs = button.getAttribute('aria-pressed') !== 'true';
                if (useVideoJs) {
                    if (!window.videojs)
                        return;
                    window.urbsPlyr?.destroy();
