                    window.urbsPlyr = null;
                    video = document.getElementById('urbs-plyr') || video;
                    video.classList.add('video-js', 'vjs-big-play-centered');
                    window.urbsVideoJs = videojs(video, { controls: true, fluid: true, preload: 'none' });
                    button.setAttribute('aria-pressed', 'true');
                    button.textContent = 'Voltar ao Plyr ↗';
                }
                else {
                    window.urbsVideoJs?.dispose();
                    window.urbsVideoJs = null;
                    // Video.js dispose() removes the original video node. Recreate it before initializing Plyr.
                    const area = document.querySelector('.media-preview');
                    area.innerHTML = '<video id="urbs-plyr" playsinline controls preload="none" poster="assets/images/urbs-motion-poster.jpg" aria-label="Animação conceitual do URBS"><source src="assets/media/urbs-movimento.mp4" type="video/mp4"></video>';
                    if (window.Plyr)
                        window.urbsPlyr = new Plyr('#urbs-plyr', { controls: ['play-large', 'play', 'progress', 'current-time', 'mute', 'volume', 'fullscreen'] });
                    button.setAttribute('aria-pressed', 'false');
                    button.textContent = 'Usar Video.js neste vídeo ↗';
                }
            });
        });
        // Deliberately no fabricated YouTube ID. Configure only a real, relevant video.
        safe('Lite YouTube', () => {
            const youtubeVideoId = ''; // Put a verified URBS or Montes Claros video ID here.
            if (!/^[\w-]{11}$/.test(youtubeVideoId))
                return;
            const slot = document.querySelector('#urbs-youtube-slot');
            if (!slot)
                return;
            const element = document.createElement('lite-youtube');
            element.setAttribute('videoid', youtubeVideoId);
            element.setAttribute('playlabel', 'Reproduzir vídeo sobre o URBS');
            slot.appendChild(element);
            slot.hidden = false;
        });
    };
    if (document.readyState === 'loading')
        document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', ready, { once: true });
    else
        ready();
})();


===== ARQUIVO: public\assets\js\preview-map.js =====
document.addEventListener("DOMContentLoaded", () => {
    const element = document.getElementById("home-map");
    if (!element || !window.L || !window.UrbsData)
        return;
    const center = [-16.7286, -43.8578];
    const colors = {
        "Pavimentação": "#b77c43",
        "Iluminação pública": "#799e32",
        "Acessibilidade": "#387f79",
        "Descarte irregular de resíduos": "#77619a",
        "Drenagem": "#4f7ca3",
        "Sinalização": "#bd7b4a",
        "Outro": "#637269"
    };
    const map = L.map(element, {
        zoomControl: false,
        scrollWheelZoom: false,
        dragging: false,
        doubleClickZoom: false,
        keyboard: false,
        attributionControl: true
    }).setView(center, 13);
    L.tileLayer("https://tile.openstreetmap.org/{z}/{x}/{y}.png", {
        maxZoom: 19,
        attribution: '&copy; <a href="https://www.openstreetmap.org/copyright">OpenStreetMap</a>'
    }).addTo(map);
    const markers = L.featureGroup().addTo(map);
    window.UrbsData.getAllReports().forEach((report) => {
        const marker = L.circleMarker([report.lat, report.lng], {
            radius: 7,
            color: "#0b1a14",
            weight: 2,
            fillColor: colors[report.category] || colors.Outro,
            fillOpacity: .95
        });
        marker.addTo(markers);
    });
    if (markers.getBounds().isValid())
        map.fitBounds(markers.getBounds().pad(.22), { maxZoom: 13 });
    window.setTimeout(() => map.invalidateSize(), 120);
});


===== ARQUIVO: public\assets\js\registro.js =====
document.addEventListener("DOMContentLoaded", async () => {
    const mapElement = document.getElementById("picker-map");
    const form = document.getElementById("report-form");
    const feedback = document.getElementById("form-feedback");
    const latitudeInput = document.getElementById("latitude");
    const longitudeInput = document.getElementById("longitude");
    const coordinates = document.getElementById("coordinates");
    const useLocation = document.getElementById("use-location");
    const description = document.getElementById("description");
    const descriptionCount = document.getElementById("description-count");
    const categorySelect = document.getElementById("category");
    const submitButton = form?.querySelector('button[type="submit"]');
    const data = window.UrbsData;
    if (!mapElement || !form || !data)
        return;
    const sessionState = await data.load();
    if (!sessionState.user) {
        const signIn = document.createElement("a");
        signIn.href = "login.html";
        signIn.className = "text-link";
        signIn.textContent = "Entrar na conta";
        feedback.replaceChildren(
            document.createTextNode(sessionState.error?.message || "Entre na sua conta para registrar uma ocorrência."),
            document.createTextNode(" "),
            signIn
        );
        feedback.className = "form-feedback is-error";
        submitButton?.setAttribute("disabled", "");
        return;
    }
    const params = new URLSearchParams(window.location.search);
    const editId = params.get("editar");
    const isEditing = editId !== null;
    const existing = isEditing ? data.getReport(editId) : null;
    if (isEditing) {
        document.title = "Editar ocorrência — URBS";
        document.getElementById("report-page-title").textContent = "Editar ocorrência";
        document.getElementById("report-submit-label").textContent = "Salvar alterações";
        if (!existing || !data.canEdit(existing)) {
            submitButton.disabled = true;
            form.addEventListener("submit", (event) => event.preventDefault());
            feedback.textContent = existing
                ? "Você só pode editar suas próprias ocorrências ainda não analisadas."
                : "Esta ocorrência não está disponível na base de dados. Volte ao mapa para consultar os registros.";
            feedback.className = "form-feedback is-error";
            return;
        }
    }
    const defaultCenter = [-16.7286, -43.8578];
    const map = window.L
        ? L.map(mapElement, { scrollWheelZoom: false, zoomControl: false }).setView(defaultCenter, 13)
        : null;
    if (map) {
        L.tileLayer("https://tile.openstreetmap.org/{z}/{x}/{y}.png", {
            maxZoom: 19,
            attribution: '&copy; <a href="https://www.openstreetmap.org/copyright">OpenStreetMap</a> contributors'
        }).addTo(map);
        L.control.zoom({ position: "bottomright" }).addTo(map);
    }
    else {
        coordinates.textContent = "O mapa não carregou. Você pode tentar novamente após recarregar a página.";
        if (useLocation)
            useLocation.disabled = true;
    }
    let marker = null;
    let saved = false;
    window.addEventListener("pageshow", (event) => {
        if (event.persisted && saved) {
            saved = false;
            submitButton.disabled = false;
            feedback.textContent = "";
            feedback.className = "form-feedback";
        }
    });
    function setPoint(latlng) {
        if (map) {
            if (marker)
                marker.setLatLng(latlng);
            else
                marker = L.marker(latlng, { draggable: true, title: "Localização da ocorrência" }).addTo(map);
            marker.off("dragend").on("dragend", () => setPoint(marker.getLatLng()));
        }
        latitudeInput.value = String(latlng.lat);
        longitudeInput.value = String(latlng.lng);
        const label = map ? "Ponto marcado" : "Ponto mantido";
        coordinates.textContent = `${label} · ${Number(latlng.lat).toFixed(6)}, ${Number(latlng.lng).toFixed(6)}`;
        coordinates.classList.add("is-picked");
        feedback.textContent = "";
        feedback.className = "form-feedback";
    }
    map?.on("click", (event) => setPoint(event.latlng));
    if (map)
        window.setTimeout(() => map.invalidateSize(), 120);
    if (existing) {
        document.getElementById("title").value = existing.title;
        document.getElementById("address").value = existing.address;
        description.value = existing.description;
        descriptionCount.textContent = String(description.value.length);
        if (![...categorySelect.options].some((option) => option.value === existing.category)) {
            const option = document.createElement("option");
            option.value = existing.category;
            option.textContent = existing.category;
            categorySelect.append(option);
        }
        categorySelect.value = existing.category;
        setPoint({ lat: existing.lat, lng: existing.lng });
        map?.setView([existing.lat, existing.lng], 16);
        if (!map)
            feedback.textContent = "O mapa não carregou. Você pode salvar as alterações mantendo o ponto atual.";
    }
    else {
        const requestedCategory = params.get("categoria");
        if (requestedCategory && [...categorySelect.options].some((option) => option.value === requestedCategory))
            categorySelect.value = requestedCategory;
    }
    description?.addEventListener("input", () => {
        if (descriptionCount)
            descriptionCount.textContent = String(description.value.length);
    });
    useLocation?.addEventListener("click", () => {
        if (!map)
            return;
        if (!navigator.geolocation) {
            feedback.textContent = "A geolocalização não está disponível neste dispositivo. Selecione um ponto no mapa.";
            feedback.className = "form-feedback is-error";
            return;
        }
        useLocation.disabled = true;
        useLocation.setAttribute("aria-busy", "true");
        feedback.textContent = "Buscando sua localização…";
        feedback.className = "form-feedback";
        navigator.geolocation.getCurrentPosition((position) => {
            const point = L.latLng(position.coords.latitude, position.coords.longitude);
            map.setView(point, 16);
            setPoint(point);
            feedback.textContent = "Localização selecionada. Confira o ponto no mapa antes de salvar.";
            useLocation.disabled = false;
            useLocation.removeAttribute("aria-busy");
        }, () => {
            feedback.textContent = "Não foi possível acessar sua localização. Confira as permissões do dispositivo ou marque o ponto no mapa.";
            feedback.className = "form-feedback is-error";
            useLocation.disabled = false;
            useLocation.removeAttribute("aria-busy");
        }, { enableHighAccuracy: true, timeout: 10000, maximumAge: 60000 });
    });
    form.addEventListener("submit", async (event) => {
        event.preventDefault();
        if (saved || !form.reportValidity())
            return;
        if (!latitudeInput.value || !longitudeInput.value) {
            feedback.textContent = map
                ? "Selecione um ponto no mapa antes de salvar."
                : "O mapa não carregou. Recarregue a página para marcar a localização.";
            feedback.className = "form-feedback is-error";
            coordinates.setAttribute("tabindex", "-1");
            coordinates.focus();
            return;
        }
        const formData = new FormData(form);
        const fields = {
            title: String(formData.get("title") || "").trim(),
            category: String(formData.get("category") || "Outro"),
            address: String(formData.get("address") || "").trim(),
            description: String(formData.get("description") || "").trim(),
            lat: Number(formData.get("latitude")),
            lng: Number(formData.get("longitude"))
        };
        if (!fields.title || !fields.description) {
            feedback.textContent = "Preencha o título e a descrição da ocorrência.";
            feedback.className = "form-feedback is-error";
            document.getElementById(fields.title ? "description" : "title").focus();
            return;
        }
        submitButton.disabled = true;
        const result = await (isEditing ? data.updateReport(editId, fields) : data.saveReport(fields));
        if (!result.ok) {
            const messages = {
                missing: "Esta ocorrência não está mais disponível. Volte ao mapa para consultar os registros.",
                auth: "Sua sessão expirou. Entre novamente para continuar.",
                server: result.message || "Não foi possível salvar a ocorrência no banco de dados."
            };
            feedback.textContent = messages[result.reason] || result.message || "Não foi possível salvar a ocorrência.";
            feedback.className = "form-feedback is-error";
            submitButton.disabled = false;
            return;
        }
        saved = true;
        feedback.textContent = isEditing
            ? "Alterações salvas no banco de dados. Abrindo a ocorrência no mapa…"
            : "Registro salvo no banco de dados. Abrindo o mapa…";
        feedback.className = "form-feedback is-success";
        const destination = new URLSearchParams({ [isEditing ? "editado" : "novo"]: result.report.id });
        window.setTimeout(() => { window.location.href = `mapa.html?${destination}`; }, 700);
    });
});




===== ARQUIVO: public\assets\js\session-ui.js =====
document.addEventListener("DOMContentLoaded", async () => {
  const nav = document.querySelector(".app-header nav");
  if (!nav || !window.UrbsAPI)
    return;

  try {
    const session = await window.UrbsAPI.session();
    if (!session.authenticated)
      return;

    const logout = document.createElement("a");
    logout.href = "#sair";
    logout.textContent = "Sair";
    logout.setAttribute("aria-label", `Sair da conta de ${session.user.name}`);
    logout.addEventListener("click", async (event) => {
      event.preventDefault();
      logout.setAttribute("aria-busy", "true");
      try {
        await window.UrbsAPI.logout();
        window.location.assign("login.html");
      } catch (error) {
        logout.removeAttribute("aria-busy");
        window.alert(error.message);
      }
    });
    nav.append(logout);
  } catch (error) {
    console.warn("Não foi possível consultar a sessão do URBS.", error.message);
  }
});


===== ARQUIVO: public\assets\js\store.js =====
(() => {
  "use strict";

  const STATUSES = ["Novo", "Em análise", "Em andamento", "Solucionado", "Rejeitado"];
  const STATUS_TO_API = {
    "Novo": "pending",
    "Em análise": "in_analysis",
    "Em andamento": "in_progress",
    "Solucionado": "resolved",
    "Rejeitado": "rejected",
  };
  const STATUS_FROM_API = Object.fromEntries(Object.entries(STATUS_TO_API).map(([label, value]) => [value, label]));
  const DEMO_REPORTS = [
    { id: "demo-pavimento", title: "Pavimentação — exemplo", category: "Pavimentação", address: "Área central · ponto demonstrativo", description: "Registro fictício usado apenas para apresentar o mapa.", lat: -16.7248, lng: -43.8631, status: "Em análise", createdAt: "2026-11-10T12:00:00.000Z", demo: true },
    { id: "demo-iluminacao", title: "Iluminação pública — exemplo", category: "Iluminação pública", address: "Setor urbano · ponto demonstrativo", description: "Exemplo fictício. Não representa uma ocorrência real.", lat: -16.7315, lng: -43.8483, status: "Em andamento", createdAt: "2026-11-08T12:00:00.000Z", demo: true },
    { id: "demo-acessibilidade", title: "Calçada e acessibilidade — exemplo", category: "Acessibilidade", address: "Setor urbano · ponto demonstrativo", description: "Ponto ilustrativo para mostrar categoria e acompanhamento.", lat: -16.7169, lng: -43.8736, status: "Novo", createdAt: "2026-11-06T12:00:00.000Z", demo: true },
    { id: "demo-residuos", title: "Descarte de resíduos — exemplo", category: "Descarte irregular de resíduos", address: "Setor urbano · ponto demonstrativo", description: "Ponto ilustrativo; os dados não correspondem a um chamado real.", lat: -16.7392, lng: -43.8674, status: "Solucionado", createdAt: "2026-11-03T12:00:00.000Z", demo: true },
    { id: "demo-drenagem", title: "Drenagem urbana — exemplo", category: "Drenagem", address: "Setor urbano · ponto demonstrativo", description: "Exemplo de demonstração para filtros e marcadores.", lat: -16.7203, lng: -43.8408, status: "Novo", createdAt: "2026-10-30T12:00:00.000Z", demo: true },
  ];

  let currentUser = null;
  let reports = [];
  let categories = [];

  function fromApi(row) {
    return {
      id: String(row.id),
      userId: Number(row.user_id),
      title: String(row.title || "Ocorrência sem título"),
      category: String(row.category_name || "Outro"),
      address: String(row.address || ""),
      description: String(row.description || ""),
      lat: Number(row.latitude),
      lng: Number(row.longitude),
      status: STATUS_FROM_API[row.status] || "Novo",
      createdAt: row.created_at || new Date().toISOString(),
      demo: false,
    };
  }

  async function loadOccurrences() {
    const result = await window.UrbsAPI.request("occurrences/list.php");
    reports = (result.data || []).map(fromApi);
    return reports;
  }

  async function load() {
    try {
      const session = await window.UrbsAPI.session();
      currentUser = session.authenticated ? session.user : null;
      if (!currentUser) {
        reports = [];
        return { user: null, error: null };
      }
      const [categoryResult] = await Promise.all([
        window.UrbsAPI.request("categories/list.php"),
        loadOccurrences(),
      ]);
      categories = categoryResult.data || [];
      return { user: currentUser, error: null };
    } catch (error) {
      currentUser = null;
      reports = [];
      return { user: null, error };
    }
  }

  async function categoryIdFor(categoryName) {
    if (!categories.length) {
      const result = await window.UrbsAPI.request("categories/list.php");
      categories = result.data || [];
    }
    const category = categories.find((item) => item.name === categoryName);
    if (!category)
      throw new Error("A categoria selecionada não está configurada no banco de dados. Importe database/urbs.sql.");
    return Number(category.id);
  }

  function canEdit(report) {
    return Boolean(currentUser && !report.demo && (
      currentUser.role === "master" ||
      (Number(currentUser.id) === report.userId && report.status === "Novo")
    ));
  }

  function canManageStatus() {
    return Boolean(currentUser && ["resolver", "master"].includes(currentUser.role));
  }

  function getAllReports() {
    return [...DEMO_REPORTS, ...reports];
  }

  function getReport(id) {
    return reports.find((report) => report.id === String(id)) || null;
  }

  function getResultError(error) {
    return { ok: false, reason: error.status === 401 ? "auth" : "server", message: error.message };
  }

  async function saveReport(fields) {
    try {
      const category_id = await categoryIdFor(fields.category);
      const result = await window.UrbsAPI.request("occurrences/create.php", {
        method: "POST",
        body: {
          title: fields.title,
          description: fields.description,
          address: fields.address || "",
          category_id,
          latitude: fields.lat,
          longitude: fields.lng,
        },
      });
      await loadOccurrences();
      return { ok: true, report: getReport(result.occurrence_id) || { id: String(result.occurrence_id) } };
    } catch (error) {
      return getResultError(error);
    }
  }

  async function updateReport(id, fields) {
    try {
      const category_id = await categoryIdFor(fields.category);
      await window.UrbsAPI.request("occurrences/update.php", {
        method: "POST",
        body: {
          occurrence_id: Number(id),
          title: fields.title,
          description: fields.description,
          address: fields.address || "",
          category_id,
          latitude: fields.lat,
          longitude: fields.lng,
        },
      });
      await loadOccurrences();
      return { ok: true, report: getReport(id) };
    } catch (error) {
      return getResultError(error);
    }
  }

  async function deleteReport(id) {
    try {
      await window.UrbsAPI.request("occurrences/delete.php", {
        method: "POST",
        body: { occurrence_id: Number(id) },
      });
      await loadOccurrences();
      return { ok: true };
    } catch (error) {
      return getResultError(error);
    }
  }

  async function updateStatus(id, label) {
    try {
      const status = STATUS_TO_API[label];
      if (!status)
        throw new Error("A situação selecionada não é válida.");
      await window.UrbsAPI.request("occurrences/update_status.php", {
        method: "POST",
        body: { occurrence_id: Number(id), status },
      });
      await loadOccurrences();
      return { ok: true };
    } catch (error) {
      return getResultError(error);
    }
  }

  window.UrbsData = Object.freeze({
    STATUSES,
    DEMO_REPORTS,
    load,
    loadOccurrences,
    getAllReports,
    getReport,
    saveReport,
    updateReport,
    deleteReport,
    updateStatus,
    canEdit,
    canManageStatus,
    formatDate(value) {
      return new Intl.DateTimeFormat("pt-BR", { dateStyle: "medium" }).format(new Date(value));
    },
    get currentUser() {
      return currentUser;
    },
  });
})();


===== ARQUIVO: public\assets\js\urban-split-v17.js =====
/* URBS V18 — acordeão V17 + relatos em modal editorial acessível e animado. */
(() => {
    'use strict';
    const root = document.getElementById('splitAccordion');
    if (!root)
        return;
    const panels = [...root.querySelectorAll('.split-panel')];
    const modal = document.getElementById('urbs-story-modal');
    if (!modal || !panels.length)
        return;
    const dialog = modal.querySelector('.urbs-story-modal__dialog');
    const closeButton = modal.querySelector('.urbs-story-modal__close');
    const pixels = modal.querySelector('.urbs-story-modal__pixels');
    const reduce = matchMedia('(prefers-reduced-motion: reduce)').matches;
    const finePointer = matchMedia('(hover: hover) and (pointer: fine)').matches;
    let locked = panels[0], modalOpen = false, returnFocus = null, closeTimer = 0;
    function select(panel, { lock = false, focus = false } = {}) {
        if (!panel || !panels.includes(panel))
            return;
        if (lock)
            locked = panel;
        panels.forEach(el => {
            const active = el === panel;
            el.classList.toggle('is-active', active);
            el.setAttribute('aria-expanded', String(active));
            const body = el.querySelector('.split-panel__content');
            if (body)
                body.inert = !active;
            el.querySelectorAll('.split-panel__story-button,.split-panel__action').forEach(btn => btn.tabIndex = active ? 0 : -1);
        });
        if (focus)
            panel.focus({ preventScroll: true });
    }
    function fillModal(panel) {
        const get = s => panel.querySelector(s)?.textContent.trim() || '';
        modal.querySelector('#urbs-story-number').textContent = get('.split-panel__meta b');
        modal.querySelector('#urbs-story-sector').textContent = get('.split-panel__kicker');
        modal.querySelector('#urbs-story-title').textContent = get('h3');
        modal.querySelector('#urbs-story-description').textContent = get('.split-panel__content p');
        const fields = panel.querySelectorAll('.split-panel__coords small');
        modal.querySelector('#urbs-story-coords').textContent = fields[0]?.textContent.trim() || '—';
        modal.querySelector('#urbs-story-status').textContent = fields[1]?.textContent.trim() || '—';
    }
    function makePixels() {
        pixels.replaceChildren();
        if (reduce)
            return;
        const fragment = document.createDocumentFragment();
        for (let i = 0; i < 96; i++) {
            const tile = document.createElement('i');
            const col = i % 12, row = Math.floor(i / 12);
            const deterministic = ((col * 17 + row * 31) % 29) / 29;
            tile.style.setProperty('--pixel-delay', (0.04 + Math.abs(col - 5.5) * .026 + deterministic * .21).toFixed(3) + 's');
            tile.style.setProperty('--pixel-rotate', ((i % 2 ? 1 : -1) * (18 + deterministic * 52)).toFixed(1) + 'deg');
            fragment.appendChild(tile);
        }
        pixels.appendChild(fragment);
    }
    function openStory(panel, trigger) {
        clearTimeout(closeTimer);
        fillModal(panel);
        returnFocus = trigger || panel;
        makePixels();
        modal.hidden = false;
        modalOpen = true;
        document.documentElement.classList.add('urbs-modal-open');
        // Ensure a transition from the closed state in all browsers.
        requestAnimationFrame(() => requestAnimationFrame(() => modal.classList.add('is-open')));
        closeButton.focus({ preventScroll: true });
    }
    function closeStory() {
        if (!modalOpen)
            return;
        modalOpen = false;
        modal.classList.remove('is-open');
        document.documentElement.classList.remove('urbs-modal-open');
        const previous = returnFocus;
        closeTimer = setTimeout(() => { if (!modalOpen) {
            modal.hidden = true;
            pixels.replaceChildren();
        } }, reduce ? 0 : 460);
        if (previous?.isConnected)
            previous.focus({ preventScroll: true });
    }
    panels.forEach((panel, index) => {
        if (finePointer)
            panel.addEventListener('pointerenter', () => { if (!modalOpen)
                select(panel); });
        panel.addEventListener('click', event => {
            if (event.target.closest('a'))
                return;
            if (event.target.closest('.split-panel__story-button')) {
                openStory(panel, event.target.closest('button'));
                return;
            }
            // Hover opens the panel; clicking the already expanded panel shows its story.
            const wasActive = panel.classList.contains('is-active');
            select(panel, { lock: true });
            if (wasActive)
                openStory(panel, panel);
        });
        panel.addEventListener('focus', event => { if (event.target === panel && !modalOpen)
            select(panel, { lock: true }); });
        panel.addEventListener('keydown', event => {
            if (event.target !== panel || modalOpen)
                return;
            if (event.key === 'Enter' || event.key === ' ') {
                event.preventDefault();
                const active = panel.classList.contains('is-active');
                select(panel, { lock: true });
                if (active)
                    openStory(panel, panel);
            }
            else if (['ArrowRight', 'ArrowDown', 'ArrowLeft', 'ArrowUp', 'Home', 'End'].includes(event.key)) {
                event.preventDefault();
                const n = event.key === 'Home' ? 0 : event.key === 'End' ? panels.length - 1 : (index + (event.key === 'ArrowLeft' || event.key === 'ArrowUp' ? -1 : 1) + panels.length) % panels.length;
                select(panels[n], { lock: true, focus: true });
            }
        });
    });
    if (finePointer)
        root.addEventListener('pointerleave', () => { if (!modalOpen)
            select(locked); });
    closeButton.addEventListener('click', closeStory);
    modal.querySelector('.urbs-story-modal__backdrop').addEventListener('click', closeStory);
    document.addEventListener('keydown', event => {
        if (!modalOpen)
            return;
        if (event.key === 'Escape') {
            event.preventDefault();
            closeStory();
            return;
        }
        if (event.key !== 'Tab')
            return;
        const focusable = [...dialog.querySelectorAll('button:not([disabled]),a[href]')].filter(el => el.getClientRects().length);
        if (!focusable.length) {
            event.preventDefault();
            dialog.focus();
            return;
        }
        const first = focusable[0], last = focusable[focusable.length - 1];
        if (event.shiftKey && document.activeElement === first) {
            event.preventDefault();
            last.focus();
        }
        else if (!event.shiftKey && document.activeElement === last) {
            event.preventDefault();
            first.focus();
        }
    });
    select(locked, { lock: true });
})();


===== ARQUIVO: public\assets\js\urbs-atlas-v19-3.js =====
/* URBS / ATLAS 19.3 - Vista cartografica 2D real e cidade 3D sob demanda.
 * Nao declara ocorrencias reais nem inventa estatisticas municipais.
 */
(() => {
    'use strict';
    const stage = document.getElementById('city-stage');
    const elMap = document.getElementById('atlas-real-map');
    if (!stage || !elMap)
        return;
    const $ = id => document.getElementById(id);
    const btn2d = $('atlas-view-2d'), btn3d = $('atlas-view-3d');
    const settings = $('atlas-settings'), offline = $('atlas-map-offline');
    const card = $('atlas-map-card');
    const center = [-16.7286, -43.8582];
    let map, markers, satellite, roads, labels, mode = '2d', currentLayer = 'satellite';
    let twinPromise = null;
    const reportData = window.UrbsData && typeof window.UrbsData.getAllReports === 'function'
        ? window.UrbsData.getAllReports() : [];
    function updateButtons() {
        btn2d.classList.toggle('is-active', mode === '2d');
        btn3d.classList.toggle('is-active', mode === '3d');
        btn2d.setAttribute('aria-pressed', String(mode === '2d'));
        btn3d.setAttribute('aria-pressed', String(mode === '3d'));
    }
    function activateMode(next) {
        if (next === mode)
            return;
        mode = next;
        stage.dataset.atlasMode = mode;
        document.querySelector('.atlas-toolbar')?.classList.toggle('is-3d', mode === '3d');
        updateButtons();
        if (mode === '2d') {
            settings.open = false;
            window.setTimeout(() => map?.invalidateSize({ pan: false }), 60);
        }
        else {
            // Carregamento sob demanda reduz o custo grafico no notebook do curso.
            if (!twinPromise) {
                const loading = $('twin-loading');
                if (loading)
                    loading.hidden = false;
                stage.dataset.twinState = 'loading';
                twinPromise = import('./urbs-digital-twin-3d-v19-2.js').catch(err => {
                    console.warn('[URBS] Three.js nao carregou:', err);
                    if (loading) {
                        loading.hidden = false;
                        loading.querySelector('strong').textContent = 'MAQUETE 3D INDISPONÍVEL';
                        loading.querySelector('small').textContent = 'Confira a conexão e o WebGL. O satélite continua disponível.';
                        loading.querySelector('.twin-loading-orbit')?.remove();
                    }
                    stage.dataset.twinState = 'error';
                });
            }
        }
    }
    btn2d.addEventListener('click', () => activateMode('2d'));
    btn3d.addEventListener('click', () => activateMode('3d'));
    document.addEventListener('keydown', event => {
        if (event.key === 'Escape' && settings.open)
            settings.open = false;
    });
    $('atlas-inspector-close')?.addEventListener('click', () => {
        const aside = $('twin-inspector');
        if (aside)
            aside.hidden = true;
    });
    function showReport(report) {
        if (!report)
            return;
        $('atlas-card-category').textContent = `${report.category || 'INFRAESTRUTURA'} / ${report.demo ? 'EXEMPLO FICTÍCIO' : 'REGISTRO LOCAL'}`;
        $('atlas-card-title').textContent = report.title || 'Registro urbano';
        $('atlas-card-description').textContent = report.description || 'Sem descrição.';
        $('atlas-card-status').textContent = `SITUAÇÃO: ${report.status || 'Novo'} · ${Number(report.lat).toFixed(4)}, ${Number(report.lng).toFixed(4)}`;
        card.hidden = false;
    }
    $('atlas-card-close').addEventListener('click', () => { card.hidden = true; });
    function initMap() {
        if (!window.L) {
            offline.hidden = false;
            return;
        }
        map = L.map(elMap, {
            zoomControl: false, scrollWheelZoom: false, attributionControl: true,
            tap: false, preferCanvas: true
        }).setView(center, 14);
        const attEsri = 'Imagens &copy; Esri, Maxar, Earthstar Geographics';
        satellite = L.tileLayer('https://server.arcgisonline.com/ArcGIS/rest/services/World_Imagery/MapServer/tile/{z}/{y}/{x}', { maxZoom: 19, attribution: attEsri });
        labels = L.tileLayer('https://server.arcgisonline.com/ArcGIS/rest/services/Reference/World_Boundaries_and_Places/MapServer/tile/{z}/{y}/{x}', { maxZoom: 19, attribution: 'Rótulos &copy; Esri', opacity: .75 });
        roads = L.tileLayer('https://tile.openstreetmap.org/{z}/{x}/{y}.png', { maxZoom: 19, attribution: '&copy; <a href="https://www.openstreetmap.org/copyright">OpenStreetMap</a>' });
        satellite.addTo(map);
        labels.addTo(map);
        let satelliteLoaded = false, roadsLoaded = false;
        satellite.on('tileload', () => { satelliteLoaded = true; offline.hidden = true; });
        roads.on('tileload', () => { roadsLoaded = true; offline.hidden = true; });
        window.setTimeout(() => {
            if (!satelliteLoaded && currentLayer === 'satellite')
                setLayer('streets');
            window.setTimeout(() => {
                if (!satelliteLoaded && !roadsLoaded)
                    offline.hidden = false;
            }, 7000);
        }, 8500);
        L.control.zoom({ position: 'topright' }).addTo(map);
        // Um unico fallback: se o provedor de satelite falhar, tente ruas.
        let tileErrors = 0;
        satellite.on('tileerror', () => {
            if (++tileErrors >= 7 && currentLayer === 'satellite')
                setLayer('streets');
        });
        const switchLayer = layer => {
            const isSatellite = layer === 'satellite';
            $('atlas-satellite').classList.toggle('is-active', isSatellite);
            $('atlas-streets').classList.toggle('is-active', !isSatellite);
            $('atlas-satellite').setAttribute('aria-pressed', String(isSatellite));
            $('atlas-streets').setAttribute('aria-pressed', String(!isSatellite));
        };
        function setLayer(layer) {
            currentLayer = layer;
            map.removeLayer(satellite);
            map.removeLayer(labels);
            map.removeLayer(roads);
            if (layer === 'satellite') {
                satellite.addTo(map);
                labels.addTo(map);
            }
            else
                roads.addTo(map);
            switchLayer(layer);
        }
        $('atlas-satellite').addEventListener('click', () => { tileErrors = 0; setLayer('satellite'); });
        $('atlas-streets').addEventListener('click', () => setLayer('streets'));
        $('atlas-center').addEventListener('click', () => { map.flyTo(center, 14, { duration: 1.0 }); card.hidden = true; });
        markers = L.layerGroup().addTo(map);
        reportData.forEach(report => {
            const lat = Number(report.lat), lng = Number(report.lng);
            if (!Number.isFinite(lat) || !Number.isFinite(lng))
                return;
            const marker = L.marker([lat, lng], {
                icon: L.divIcon({ className: 'atlas-map-marker', html: `<span class="atlas-map-pin${report.demo ? '' : ' atlas-map-pin--local'}" aria-hidden="true"></span>`, iconSize: [30, 30], iconAnchor: [15, 15] }),
                title: `${report.category || 'Registro'} · ${report.demo ? 'Exemplo fictício' : 'Registro local'}`
            });
            marker.addTo(markers);
            marker.on('click', () => { showReport(report); map.panTo([lat, lng], { animate: true, duration: .7 }); });
        });
        // A camada 2D deve caber no palco independente da pagina.
        const resizeObserver = ('ResizeObserver' in window) ? new ResizeObserver(() => { if (mode === '2d')
            map.invalidateSize({ pan: false }); }) : null;
        resizeObserver?.observe(elMap);
        window.addEventListener('pagehide', () => resizeObserver?.disconnect(), { once: true });
        setTimeout(() => map.invalidateSize(), 150);
    }
    // Leaflet pode estar bloqueado por rede ou firewall da escola.
    if (document.readyState === 'loading')
        document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', initMap, { once: true });
    else
        initMap();
})();


===== ARQUIVO: public\assets\js\urbs-before-after-v20.js =====
/* URBS V20 — comparador com quatro intervenções reais documentadas.
   As fontes fornecem composições de duas fotografias empilhadas verticalmente.
   O recorte em CSS permite sobrepor os dois estados sem produzir imagens fictícias.
*/
(() => {
    'use strict';
    // Frações verticais da montagem que excluem as legendas impressas.
    const curitibaFrames = { before: [.055, .495], after: [.555, .995] };
    const cases = [
        {
            title: 'Pavimentação de vias locais',
            thumbnail: 'ASFALTO',
            category: 'PAVIMENTAÇÃO',

            photo: 'https://mid-noticias.curitiba.pr.gov.br/2024/00441623.jpg',
            frames: curitibaFrames,
            source: 'https://www.curitiba.pr.gov.br/noticias/antes-e-depois-veja-como-ficou-rua-de-curitiba-transformada-por-obra-da-prefeitura/73095',
            metrics: [['EXTENSÃO', '90 m'], ['INTERVENÇÃO', 'Asfalto'], ['STATUS', 'Concluída']]
        },
        {
            title: 'Escoamento da água da chuva',
            thumbnail: 'DRENAGEM',
            category: 'DRENAGEM E PAVIMENTAÇÃO',
            location: 'CURITIBA / PR · LAMENHA PEQUENA',
            description: 'A Rua Lionidetti Sovinski Pacheco passou de saibro a asfalto. A obra incluiu galerias pluviais para escoar a água da chuva.',
            photo: 'https://mid-noticias.curitiba.pr.gov.br/2024/00433866.jpg',
            frames: curitibaFrames,
            source: 'https://www.curitiba.pr.gov.br/noticias/antes-e-depois-rua-de-curitiba-e-transformada-com-asfalto-e-sistema-de-drenagem/72588',
            metrics: [['ANTES', 'Saibro'], ['DEPOIS', 'Asfalto'], ['STATUS', 'Concluída']]
        },
        {
            title: 'Calçadas e saneamento',
            thumbnail: 'CALÇADAS',
            category: 'URBANIZAÇÃO',
            location: 'FORTALEZA / CE',
            description: 'Na Rua Expedito Madruga Monte, a urbanização reuniu pavimentação, calçadas e obras de saneamento.',
            photo: 'assets/images/comparacoes/expedito-madruga-monte.jpg',
            frames: { before: [.04, .44], after: [.60, 1], width: .72 },
            source: 'https://www.fortaleza.ce.gov.br/noticias/obras-de-drenagem-e-saneamento-vao-beneficiar-65-ruas-no-canindezinho',
            metrics: [['TIPO', 'Urbanização'], ['REGIÃO', 'Fortaleza'], ['FONTE', 'Prefeitura']]
        },
        {
            title: 'Pavimentação e drenagem integradas',
            thumbnail: 'PAVIMENTAÇÃO',
            category: 'PAVIMENTAÇÃO E DRENAGEM',
            location: 'CURITIBA / PR · UMBARÁ',
            description: 'Na Rua Manoel Miguel Alves Filho, 742 metros receberam pavimentação, meio-fio e galerias pluviais.',
            photo: 'https://mid-noticias.curitiba.pr.gov.br/2024/capa/00440018.jpg',
            frames: curitibaFrames,
            source: 'https://www.curitiba.pr.gov.br/noticias/prefeitura-de-curitiba-troca-saibro-pelo-asfalto-e-melhora-a-vida-de-moradores-do-umbara/72945',
            metrics: [['EXTENSÃO', '742 m'], ['INTERVENÇÃO', 'Drenagem'], ['STATUS', 'Concluída']]
        }
    ];
    const byId = (id) => document.getElementById(id);
    const viewer = byId('urb-ba-viewer');
    if (!viewer)
        return;
    const range = byId('urb-ba-slider');
    const before = byId('urb-ba-before');
    const after = byId('urb-ba-after');
    const thumbs = byId('urb-ba-thumbnails');
    const error = byId('urb-ba-image-error');
    let active = 0;
    let loadToken = 0;
    let animationTimeout = 0;
    const info = byId('urb-ba-details') || document.querySelector('.urb-comparison__info');
    const dialog = byId('urb-ba-dialog');
    const host = byId('urb-ba-viewer-host');
    const stage = byId('urb-ba-dialog-stage');
    const openFullscreen = byId('urb-ba-open-fullscreen');
    const updateSplit = () => viewer.style.setProperty('--urb-ba-split', `${Number(range.value)}%`);
    const presetButtons = [...document.querySelectorAll('[data-urb-ba-preset]')];
    const syncPresets = () => {
        const current = Math.round(Number(range.value));
        presetButtons.forEach((button) => {
            const pressed = current === Number(button.dataset.urbBaPreset);
            button.classList.toggle('is-active', pressed);
            button.setAttribute('aria-pressed', String(pressed));
        });
    };
    const setSplit = (position) => {
        range.value = String(position);
        updateSplit();
        syncPresets();
    };
    range.addEventListener('input', () => { updateSplit(); syncPresets(); });
    presetButtons.forEach((button) => button.addEventListener('click', () => {
        setSplit(Number(button.dataset.urbBaPreset));
        range.focus({ preventScroll: true });
    }));
    function setPhoto(element, item, side) {
        const [start, end] = item.frames[side];
        const height = end - start;
        const width = item.frames.width ?? 1;
        element.style.backgroundImage = `url("${item.photo.replaceAll('"', '%22')}")`;
        element.style.backgroundSize = `${100 / width}% ${100 / height}%`;
        element.style.backgroundPosition = `center ${(start / (1 - height) * 100).toFixed(6)}%`;
    }
    function setCase(index) {
        active = (index + cases.length) % cases.length;
        const item = cases[active];
        byId('urb-ba-title').textContent = item.title;
        byId('urb-ba-category').textContent = item.category;
        byId('urb-ba-description').textContent = item.description;
        byId('urb-ba-location').textContent = `FOTOS: ${item.location}`;
        byId('urb-ba-source').href = item.source;
        byId('urb-ba-counter').textContent = `${String(active + 1).padStart(2, '0')} / ${String(cases.length).padStart(2, '0')}`;
        item.metrics.forEach(([label, value], i) => {
            byId(`urb-ba-metric-label-${i + 1}`).textContent = label;
            byId(`urb-ba-metric-${i + 1}`).textContent = value;
        });
        setPhoto(before, item, 'before');
        setPhoto(after, item, 'after');
        clearTimeout(animationTimeout);
        viewer.classList.remove('is-transitioning');
        info?.classList.remove('is-transitioning');
        // Reinicia as animações sem afetar os demais elementos da homepage.
        void viewer.offsetWidth;
        viewer.classList.add('is-transitioning');
        info?.classList.add('is-transitioning');
        animationTimeout = window.setTimeout(() => {
            viewer.classList.remove('is-transitioning');
            info?.classList.remove('is-transitioning');
        }, 520);
        after.setAttribute('aria-label', `Imagem da condição posterior: ${item.title}.`);
        error.hidden = true;
        setSplit(50);
        byId('urb-ba-progress-fill').style.width = `${((active + 1) / cases.length) * 100}%`;
        byId('urb-ba-dialog-counter').textContent = byId('urb-ba-counter').textContent;
        byId('urb-ba-dialog-title').textContent = item.title;
        [...thumbs.children].forEach((thumb, i) => {
            thumb.classList.toggle('is-active', i === active);
            thumb.setAttribute('aria-pressed', String(i === active));
        });
        const token = ++loadToken;
        const check = new Image();
        check.onerror = () => { if (token === loadToken)
            error.hidden = false; };
        check.src = item.photo;
    }
    cases.forEach((item, index) => {
        const button = document.createElement('button');
        button.type = 'button';
        button.className = 'urb-comparison__thumb';
        button.setAttribute('aria-label', `Visualizar ${item.title}`);
        const photo = document.createElement('span');
        photo.className = 'urb-comparison__thumb-photo';
        setPhoto(photo, item, 'after');
        button.appendChild(photo);
        const label = document.createElement('span');
        label.className = 'urb-comparison__thumb-label';
        label.textContent = `${String(index + 1).padStart(2, '0')} / ${item.thumbnail}`;
        button.appendChild(label);
        button.addEventListener('click', () => setCase(index));
        thumbs.appendChild(button);
    });
    byId('urb-ba-prev').addEventListener('click', () => setCase(active - 1));
    byId('urb-ba-next').addEventListener('click', () => setCase(active + 1));
    const closeFullscreen = () => { if (dialog.open)
        dialog.close(); };
    const restoreViewer = () => {
        host.appendChild(viewer);
        openFullscreen.focus({ preventScroll: true });
    };
    openFullscreen.addEventListener('click', () => {
        if (!dialog.showModal)
            return;
        stage.appendChild(viewer);
        try {
            dialog.showModal();
            byId('urb-ba-dialog-close').focus({ preventScroll: true });
        }
        catch (error) {
            host.appendChild(viewer);
            console.warn('Não foi possível ampliar o comparador.', error);
        }
    });
    dialog.addEventListener('close', restoreViewer);
    byId('urb-ba-dialog-close').addEventListener('click', closeFullscreen);
    byId('urb-ba-dialog-prev').addEventListener('click', () => setCase(active - 1));
    byId('urb-ba-dialog-next').addEventListener('click', () => setCase(active + 1));
    dialog.addEventListener('click', (event) => {
        if (event.target === dialog)
            closeFullscreen();
    });
    dialog.addEventListener('keydown', (event) => {
        if (event.target === range)
            return; // Setas do teclado movem o divisor.
        if (event.key === 'ArrowLeft') {
            event.preventDefault();
            setCase(active - 1);
        }
        if (event.key === 'ArrowRight') {
            event.preventDefault();
            setCase(active + 1);
        }
    });
    // Troca de slides apenas por botões e miniaturas — sem swipe horizontal.
    setCase(0);
})();


===== ARQUIVO: public\assets\js\urbs-city-3d.js =====
/* URBS V12 — Cidade conceitual, percurso de câmera por rolagem e fluxos de luz.
   Dados exibidos nos tooltips são fictícios e existem apenas para demonstrar a interface.
   WebGL e módulos externos são opcionais: a imagem alternativa permanece se falharem. */
import * as THREE from 'three';
const host = document.getElementById('urban-canvas');
if (host && !matchMedia('(prefers-reduced-motion: reduce)').matches) {
    startCity().catch(error => console.warn('[URBS 3D] Cena indisponível; alternativa visual preservada.', error));
}
async function startCity() {
    const scene = new THREE.Scene();
    scene.fog = new THREE.FogExp2(0x092116, 0.023);
    const camera = new THREE.PerspectiveCamera(39, 1, 0.1, 220);
    camera.position.set(30, 27, 42);
    camera.lookAt(0, 7, 0);
    const renderer = new THREE.WebGLRenderer({ alpha: true, antialias: true, powerPreference: 'high-performance' });
    renderer.setPixelRatio(Math.min(devicePixelRatio || 1, 1.5));
    renderer.outputColorSpace = THREE.SRGBColorSpace;
    renderer.toneMapping = THREE.ACESFilmicToneMapping;
    renderer.toneMappingExposure = 1.45;
    renderer.shadowMap.enabled = true;
    renderer.shadowMap.type = THREE.PCFSoftShadowMap;
    renderer.setClearColor(0x071b12, 0);
    host.appendChild(renderer.domElement);
    const city = new THREE.Group();
    scene.add(city);
    // Grade sem base sólida: suaviza o alpha das extremidades via cor de vértice.
    const grid = new THREE.GridHelper(120, 72, 0x9bdc6f, 0x284d39);
    grid.position.y = -0.08;
    grid.material.transparent = true;
    grid.material.opacity = 0.22;
    grid.material.depthWrite = false;
    // A névoa exponencial do cenário oculta naturalmente o horizonte da grade.
    city.add(grid);
    const ambient = new THREE.HemisphereLight(0xd6ffd8, 0x0b2015, 1.95);
    scene.add(ambient);
    const baseGlow = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.CircleGeometry(24, 48), new THREE.MeshBasicMaterial({ color: 0x8bff5b, transparent: true, opacity: .06, depthWrite: false }));
    baseGlow.rotation.x = -Math.PI / 2;
    baseGlow.position.y = 0.02;
    city.add(baseGlow);
    const sun = new THREE.DirectionalLight(0xecffe0, 3.9);
    sun.position.set(22, 48, 18);
    sun.castShadow = true;
    sun.shadow.mapSize.set(1024, 1024);
    sun.shadow.camera.left = -40;
    sun.shadow.camera.right = 40;
    sun.shadow.camera.top = 40;
    sun.shadow.camera.bottom = -40;
    sun.shadow.camera.near = 1;
    sun.shadow.camera.far = 120;
    sun.shadow.bias = -0.0008;
    sun.shadow.radius = 3;
    scene.add(sun);
    const rim = new THREE.PointLight(0xa3e635, 82, 92);
    rim.position.set(-15, 24, -18);
    scene.add(rim);
    const accent = new THREE.PointLight(0x7ef6d4, 28, 60);
    accent.position.set(12, 16, 18);
    scene.add(accent);
    const buildings = [];
    let seed = 24167;
    const rand = () => ((seed = (seed * 1664525 + 1013904223) >>> 0) / 4294967296);
    const sectors = ['Norte', 'Sul', 'Leste', 'Oeste', 'Central'];
    const lineMaterial = new THREE.LineBasicMaterial({ color: 0xa3e635, transparent: true, opacity: .94, depthWrite: false });
    const glassMaterial = new THREE.MeshPhysicalMaterial({
        color: 0x7cbf8b, metalness: .04, roughness: .16, transparent: true, opacity: .13,
        transmission: .58, ior: 1.08, thickness: .9, clearcoat: .7, clearcoatRoughness: .12,
        depthWrite: false, side: THREE.DoubleSide
    });
    const rooftopMaterial = new THREE.MeshBasicMaterial({ color: 0xd7ff85, transparent: true, opacity: .95, depthWrite: false });
    const hitMaterial = new THREE.MeshBasicMaterial({ visible: false });
    const rayHits = [];
    for (let x = -20; x <= 20; x += 4) {
        for (let z = -20; z <= 20; z += 4) {
            if (x % 12 === 0 || z % 12 === 0 || rand() < .18)
                continue;
            const h = 4 + rand() * 13 + (Math.abs(x) < 9 && Math.abs(z) < 9 ? rand() * 10 : 0);
            const w = 2.1 + rand() * 1.2, d = 2.1 + rand() * 1.2;
            const bgroup = new THREE.Group();
            bgroup.position.set(x, 0, z);
            const geo = new THREE.BoxGeometry(w, h, d);
            const glass = new THREE.Mesh(geo, glassMaterial);
            glass.position.y = h / 2;
            glass.castShadow = true;
            glass.receiveShadow = true;
            bgroup.add(glass);
            const outline = new THREE.LineSegments(new THREE.EdgesGeometry(geo), lineMaterial);
            outline.position.y = h / 2;
            bgroup.add(outline);
            // LEDs de topo — somente nas torres mais altas.
            if (h > 16) {
                const led = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.BoxGeometry(w * .77, .09, d * .77), rooftopMaterial);
                led.position.y = h + .08;
                bgroup.add(led);
            }
            // Geometria invisível para seleção via raycasting.
            const hit = new THREE.Mesh(geo, hitMaterial);
            hit.position.y = h / 2;
            hit.userData.building = bgroup;
            bgroup.add(hit);
            rayHits.push(hit);
            const sector = sectors[Math.min(4, Math.floor(rand() * sectors.length))];
            bgroup.userData = { sector, density: Math.round(55 + rand() * 37), baseY: 0, hover: false, floatOffset: rand() * Math.PI * 2 };
            city.add(bgroup);
            buildings.push(bgroup);
        }
    }
    // Fluxos conceituais: linhas de circulação animadas com pontos luminosos.
    // Não representam ruas ou dados reais de Montes Claros.
    const animatedFlows = [];
    const flowLineMaterial = new THREE.LineBasicMaterial({ color: 0xb9ff75, transparent: true, opacity: .44, depthWrite: false });
    const flowDotMaterial = new THREE.MeshBasicMaterial({ color: 0xd5ff88, transparent: true, opacity: .95, depthWrite: false });
    const routes = [
        [[-23, .14, -12], [-12, .14, -12], [1, .14, -12], [21, .14, -12]],
        [[-24, .15, 12], [-12, .15, 12], [0, .15, 12], [24, .15, 12]],
        [[-12, .18, -23], [-12, .18, -5], [-12, .18, 10], [-12, .18, 23]],
        [[12, .18, -23], [12, .18, -8], [12, .18, 8], [12, .18, 23]],
        [[-22, .22, 0], [-8, .22, 0], [7, .22, 0], [22, .22, 0]]
    ];
    routes.forEach((route, index) => {
        const curve = new THREE.CatmullRomCurve3(route.map(v => new THREE.Vector3(...v)));
        const points = curve.getPoints(90);
        city.add(new THREE.Line(new THREE.BufferGeometry().setFromPoints(points), flowLineMaterial));
        const glow = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.SphereGeometry(.20, 8, 8), flowDotMaterial);
        city.add(glow);
        animatedFlows.push({ curve, glow, phase: index / routes.length });
    });
    // Aros luminosos discretos em pontos altos, cada um em um grupo existente.
    const beacons = [];
    buildings.filter(b => b.children.some(obj => obj.geometry?.type === 'BoxGeometry' && obj.position.y > 10)).slice(0, 12).forEach((building, i) => {
        const height = building.children[0].geometry.parameters.height;
        if (height < 15)
            return;
        const beacon = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.RingGeometry(.43, .51, 32), new THREE.MeshBasicMaterial({ color: 0xb6ff63, transparent: true, opacity: .38, side: THREE.DoubleSide, depthWrite: false }));
        beacon.rotation.x = -Math.PI / 2;
        beacon.position.y = height + .20;
        building.add(beacon);
        beacons.push({ beacon, index: i });
    });
    const selectPulse = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.RingGeometry(0.88, 1.08, 48), new THREE.MeshBasicMaterial({ color: 0xd8ff8d, transparent: true, opacity: 0, side: THREE.DoubleSide, depthWrite: false }));
    selectPulse.rotation.x = -Math.PI / 2;
    selectPulse.position.y = 0.18;
    city.add(selectPulse);
    // Partículas técnicas sutis no espaço, sem obstruir os edifícios.
    const positions = [];
    for (let i = 0; i < 175; i++)
        positions.push((rand() - .5) * 85, rand() * 35 + 2, (rand() - .5) * 85);
    const pointsGeom = new THREE.BufferGeometry();
    pointsGeom.setAttribute('position', new THREE.Float32BufferAttribute(positions, 3));
    const points = new THREE.Points(pointsGeom, new THREE.PointsMaterial({ color: 0xc0ff9d, size: .08, transparent: true, opacity: .52, depthWrite: false }));
    scene.add(points);
    // UnrealBloomPass em intensidade baixa; se o módulo falhar, o renderer permanece funcional.
    let composer = null;
    try {
        const [{ EffectComposer }, { RenderPass }, { UnrealBloomPass }, { OutputPass }] = await Promise.all([
            import('three/addons/postprocessing/EffectComposer.js'),
            import('three/addons/postprocessing/RenderPass.js'),
            import('three/addons/postprocessing/UnrealBloomPass.js'),
            import('three/addons/postprocessing/OutputPass.js')
        ]);
        composer = new EffectComposer(renderer);
        composer.addPass(new RenderPass(scene, camera));
        const bloom = new UnrealBloomPass(new THREE.Vector2(1, 1), .58, .34, .82);
        composer.addPass(bloom);
        composer.addPass(new OutputPass());
    }
    catch (e) {
        console.info('[URBS 3D] Bloom opcional indisponível.', e);
    }
    const tooltip = document.getElementById('city-tooltip');
    const raycaster = new THREE.Raycaster();
    const pointer = new THREE.Vector2(4, 4);
    let selected = null, drag = false, lastX = 0, yaw = -.18, px = 0, py = 0, visible = true, lastFrame = performance.now(), raf = 0;
    let selectedPulse = null;
    const stage = document.getElementById('city-stage');
    const immersive = document.querySelector('.immersive');
    const runway = document.querySelector('.city-scroll-runway');
    const progressBar = document.getElementById('city-progress-bar');
    const caption = document.getElementById('journey-caption');
    const title = document.getElementById('journey-title');
    const detail = document.getElementById('journey-detail');
    const route = [
        { at: 0, position: [34, 31, 49], look: [0, 7, 0], yaw: -.20 },
        { at: .31, position: [19, 17, 25], look: [0, 9, -3], yaw: .05 },
        { at: .66, position: [7, 11, 16], look: [0, 11, -11], yaw: .24 },
        { at: 1, position: [32, 34, 45], look: [0, 7, 0], yaw: .46 }
    ];
    const labels = [
        ['VISÃO DO TERRITÓRIO', 'OLHE DE CIMA.', 'A cidade revela suas conexões.'],
        ['EXPLORANDO AS CAMADAS', 'ATRAVESSE.', 'Fluxos, estruturas e movimento.'],
        ['INTELIGÊNCIA URBANA', 'APROXIME-SE.', 'Cada estrutura contém uma história.'],
        ['UMA NOVA PERSPECTIVA', 'VEJA O TODO.', 'O território se conecta novamente.']
    ];
    let scrollProgress = 0, scrollLastLabel = -1, lastManualTime = 0;
    const clamp = (n, a, b) => Math.max(a, Math.min(b, n));
    const frameRoute = t => {
        let k = 0;
        while (k < route.length - 2 && t > route[k + 1].at)
            k++;
        const a = route[k], b = route[k + 1], raw = clamp((t - a.at) / (b.at - a.at), 0, 1);
        const f = raw * raw * (3 - 2 * raw); // smooth motion through narrative camera phases
        const lerpArray = (x, y) => x.map((v, i) => THREE.MathUtils.lerp(v, y[i], f));
        return { pos: lerpArray(a.position, b.position), look: lerpArray(a.look, b.look), yaw: THREE.MathUtils.lerp(a.yaw, b.yaw, f) };
    };
    const updateScroll = () => {
        if (!stage || !runway || !immersive)
            return;
        const start = immersive.getBoundingClientRect().top + window.scrollY + stage.offsetTop;
        scrollProgress = clamp((window.scrollY - start) / Math.max(1, runway.offsetHeight), 0, 1);
        if (progressBar)
            progressBar.style.transform = `scaleX(${scrollProgress.toFixed(4)})`;
        const index = scrollProgress < .26 ? 0 : scrollProgress < .54 ? 1 : scrollProgress < .82 ? 2 : 3;
        if (index !== scrollLastLabel) {
            scrollLastLabel = index;
            caption.textContent = labels[index][0];
            title.textContent = labels[index][1];
            detail.textContent = labels[index][2];
        }
    };
    window.addEventListener('scroll', updateScroll, { passive: true });
    window.addEventListener('resize', updateScroll, { passive: true });
    const resetTooltip = () => { selected = null; if (tooltip)
        tooltip.hidden = true; };
    const mousePoint = event => {
        const rect = host.getBoundingClientRect();
        px = ((event.clientX - rect.left) / rect.width - .5) * 2;
        py = ((event.clientY - rect.top) / rect.height - .5) * 2;
        pointer.set(px, -py);
        if (tooltip) {
            tooltip.style.left = Math.min(rect.width - 210, Math.max(12, event.clientX - rect.left)) + 'px';
            tooltip.style.top = Math.max(92, event.clientY - rect.top) + 'px';
        }
    };
    host.addEventListener('pointermove', event => {
        mousePoint(event);
        if (drag) {
            yaw += (event.clientX - lastX) * .007;
            lastX = event.clientX;
            resetTooltip();
        }
    });
    host.addEventListener('pointerdown', event => {
        if (event.button !== 0)
            return;
        drag = true;
        lastManualTime = performance.now();
        lastX = event.clientX;
        host.classList.add('dragging');
        try {
            host.setPointerCapture(event.pointerId);
        }
        catch { }
    });
    const release = () => { drag = false; lastManualTime = performance.now(); host.classList.remove('dragging'); };
    host.addEventListener('pointerup', release);
    host.addEventListener('pointercancel', release);
    host.addEventListener('pointerleave', () => { pointer.set(4, 4); resetTooltip(); });
    const resize = () => {
        const w = host.clientWidth, h = host.clientHeight;
        if (!w || !h)
            return;
        camera.aspect = w / h;
        camera.updateProjectionMatrix();
        renderer.setSize(w, h, false);
        composer?.setSize(w, h);
        updateScroll();
    };
    resize();
    camera.position.set(34, 31, 49);
    camera.lookAt(0, 7, 0);
    const lookTarget = new THREE.Vector3(0, 7, 0);
    const resizeObserver = new ResizeObserver(resize);
    resizeObserver.observe(host);
    const observer = new IntersectionObserver(entries => { visible = entries[0].isIntersecting; }, { threshold: .02 });
    observer.observe(host);
    function loop(now) {
        raf = requestAnimationFrame(loop);
        if (!visible || document.hidden) {
            lastFrame = now;
            return;
        }
        const dt = Math.min((now - lastFrame) / 1000, .05);
        lastFrame = now;
        if (!drag)
            yaw += dt * .018;
        const path = frameRoute(scrollProgress);
        const narrow = host.clientWidth < 700;
        const cameraScale = narrow ? 1.25 : 1;
        const targetPosition = new THREE.Vector3(...path.pos).multiplyScalar(cameraScale);
        camera.position.lerp(targetPosition, Math.min(1, dt * 2.3));
        lookTarget.lerp(new THREE.Vector3(...path.look), Math.min(1, dt * 2.8));
        camera.lookAt(lookTarget);
        const manual = drag || now - lastManualTime < 2500;
        const desiredYaw = (manual ? yaw : path.yaw) + px * .025;
        city.rotation.y += (desiredYaw - city.rotation.y) * Math.min(1, dt * 2.5);
        city.rotation.x += (-.08 + py * .025 - city.rotation.x) * Math.min(1, dt * 3);
        animatedFlows.forEach(flow => {
            flow.phase = (flow.phase + dt * .075) % 1;
            flow.glow.position.copy(flow.curve.getPointAt(flow.phase));
        });
        beacons.forEach(({ beacon, index }) => { beacon.material.opacity = .25 + .20 * Math.sin(now * .0012 + index); });
        if (selected) {
            selectPulse.material.opacity += (0.42 - selectPulse.material.opacity) * 0.14;
            selectPulse.position.x += (selected.position.x - selectPulse.position.x) * 0.16;
            selectPulse.position.z += (selected.position.z - selectPulse.position.z) * 0.16;
            const pulseScale = 1 + 0.12 * Math.sin(now * 0.005);
            selectPulse.scale.setScalar(pulseScale);
        }
        else {
            selectPulse.material.opacity += (0 - selectPulse.material.opacity) * 0.18;
        }
        if (!drag) {
            raycaster.setFromCamera(pointer, camera);
            const hit = raycaster.intersectObjects(rayHits, false)[0]?.object?.userData.building || null;
            if (hit !== selected) {
                selected = hit;
                if (tooltip) {
                    tooltip.hidden = !hit;
                    if (hit) {
                        tooltip.querySelector('.city-tooltip-title').textContent = 'Setor ' + hit.userData.sector;
                        tooltip.querySelector('.city-tooltip-value').textContent = 'Densidade ilustrativa: ' + hit.userData.density + '%';
                    }
                }
            }
        }
        for (const building of buildings) {
            const floatBase = Math.sin(now * 0.0007 + building.userData.floatOffset) * 0.07;
            const aim = (building === selected ? 1.18 : 0) + floatBase;
            building.position.y += (aim - building.position.y) * .11;
        }
        if (composer)
            composer.render();
        else
            renderer.render(scene, camera);
    }
    updateScroll();
    raf = requestAnimationFrame(loop);
    host.parentElement.classList.add('has-3d');
    addEventListener('pagehide', () => {
        cancelAnimationFrame(raf);
        observer.disconnect();
        resizeObserver.disconnect();
        composer?.dispose();
        renderer.dispose();
        for (const hit of rayHits)
            hit.geometry.dispose();
        pointsGeom.dispose();
        window.removeEventListener('scroll', updateScroll);
        window.removeEventListener('resize', updateScroll);
        for (const flow of animatedFlows) {
            flow.glow.geometry.dispose();
        }
    }, { once: true });
}


===== ARQUIVO: public\assets\js\urbs-digital-twin-3d-v19-2.js =====
/**
 * URBS V19.2 — LIVING CITY / DIGITAL TWIN 360°
 * Cena urbana procedural, dados 100% conceituais. Navegação 360° com OrbitControls,
 * arquitetura modelada com Three.js, raycasting, panorâmica, zoom e tour.
 * Não usa API, não representa edifícios ou ocorrências reais de Montes Claros.
 */
import * as THREE from 'three';
import { OrbitControls } from 'three/addons/controls/OrbitControls.js';
const stage = document.getElementById('city-stage');
const host = document.getElementById('urban-canvas');
if (stage && host) {
    try {
        initTwin();
    }
    catch (error) {
        showFailure(error);
    }
}
function showFailure(error) {
    console.warn('[URBS Digital Twin] Visualização 3D indisponível.', error);
    const loading = document.getElementById('twin-loading');
    if (loading) {
        loading.querySelector('strong').textContent = 'VISUALIZAÇÃO 3D INDISPONÍVEL';
        loading.querySelector('small').textContent = 'Verifique o WebGL e a conexão com as bibliotecas. O mapa de ocorrências continua acessível.';
        loading.querySelector('.twin-loading-orbit')?.remove();
    }
    stage.dataset.twinState = 'error';
}
function initTwin() {
    const reducedMotion = window.matchMedia('(prefers-reduced-motion: reduce)').matches;
    const mobile = window.matchMedia('(max-width: 700px)').matches;
    const scene = new THREE.Scene();
    scene.background = new THREE.Color(0x07150f);
    scene.fog = new THREE.FogExp2(0x081d15, 0.0085);
    const camera = new THREE.PerspectiveCamera(mobile ? 41 : 39, 1, 0.1, 220);
    const homePos = new THREE.Vector3(mobile ? 61 : 49, mobile ? 59 : 42, mobile ? 64 : 54);
    const homeTarget = new THREE.Vector3(0, 1.6, 0);
    camera.position.copy(homePos);
    const renderer = new THREE.WebGLRenderer({ alpha: false, antialias: true, powerPreference: 'high-performance' });
    renderer.setPixelRatio(Math.min(window.devicePixelRatio || 1, mobile ? 1.1 : 1.6));
    renderer.outputColorSpace = THREE.SRGBColorSpace;
    renderer.toneMapping = THREE.ACESFilmicToneMapping;
    renderer.toneMappingExposure = 1.85;
    renderer.shadowMap.enabled = !mobile;
    renderer.shadowMap.type = THREE.PCFSoftShadowMap;
    renderer.setClearColor(0x07150f, 1);
    renderer.domElement.setAttribute('aria-hidden', 'true');
    host.appendChild(renderer.domElement);
    const controls = new OrbitControls(camera, renderer.domElement);
    controls.target.copy(homeTarget);
    controls.enableDamping = !reducedMotion;
    controls.dampingFactor = .065;
    controls.enablePan = false;
    controls.rotateSpeed = .57;
    controls.zoomSpeed = .78;
    controls.minDistance = mobile ? 18 : 13;
    controls.maxDistance = 110;
    controls.minPolarAngle = .13;
    controls.maxPolarAngle = Math.PI / 2.04; // 88°: permite perspectiva de rua sem atravessar o piso
    controls.enableRotate = true;
    controls.enableZoom = true;
    controls.autoRotate = !reducedMotion;
    controls.autoRotateSpeed = .18;
    controls.screenSpacePanning = false;
    controls.update();
    const hemisphere = new THREE.HemisphereLight(0xc7e1dd, 0x17271c, 2.25);
    scene.add(hemisphere);
    const ambient = new THREE.AmbientLight(0x7ba88b, .45);
    scene.add(ambient);
    const sunlight = new THREE.DirectionalLight(0xf7e9c9, 3.15);
    sunlight.position.set(36, 69, 28);
    sunlight.castShadow = !mobile;
    sunlight.shadow.mapSize.set(mobile ? 1024 : 2048, mobile ? 1024 : 2048);
    sunlight.shadow.camera.left = -45;
    sunlight.shadow.camera.right = 45;
    sunlight.shadow.camera.top = 45;
    sunlight.shadow.camera.bottom = -45;
    sunlight.shadow.camera.near = 1;
    sunlight.shadow.camera.far = 145;
    sunlight.shadow.bias = -.0004;
    sunlight.shadow.normalBias = .035;
    scene.add(sunlight);
    const edgeLight = new THREE.DirectionalLight(0xb8e954, .68);
    edgeLight.position.set(-29, 23, -32);
    scene.add(edgeLight);
    const moonLight = new THREE.DirectionalLight(0x93b9ca, 0);
    moonLight.position.set(-28, 42, -19);
    scene.add(moonLight);
    // Luz configurável: dia, entardecer e noite, com transição contínua no render.
    const lightingPresets = {
        day: { bg: 0x07150f, fog: 0x081d15, hemi: 2.25, ambient: .45, sun: 3.15, sunHex: 0xf7e9c9, hemiHex: 0xc7e1dd, sunPos: [36, 69, 28], moon: 0, edge: .68, exposure: 1.85, lamp: .18, pool: .07, window: .08, x: .15, label: 'SOL / 09:30' },
        dusk: { bg: 0x191b20, fog: 0x292921, hemi: 1.12, ambient: .31, sun: 1.52, sunHex: 0xffac72, hemiHex: 0xb3afa8, sunPos: [53, 15, -22], moon: .18, edge: .9, exposure: 1.48, lamp: 2.2, pool: .28, window: .7, x: .24, label: 'ENTARDECER / 18:15' },
        night: { bg: 0x060f1b, fog: 0x091b24, hemi: .52, ambient: .15, sun: .10, sunHex: 0x6d849c, hemiHex: 0x758ca6, sunPos: [-35, 15, -40], moon: 1.45, edge: .34, exposure: 1.30, lamp: 4.9, pool: .62, window: 1, x: .43, label: 'NOITE / 21:00' }
    };
    let timeMode = 'day';
    let xrayMode = 'all';
    let streetMode = false;
    const fogTarget = new THREE.Color(lightingPresets.day.fog);
    const skyTarget = new THREE.Color(lightingPresets.day.bg);
    const sunColorTarget = new THREE.Color(lightingPresets.day.sunHex);
    const hemiColorTarget = new THREE.Color(lightingPresets.day.hemiHex);
    const sunPosTarget = new THREE.Vector3(...lightingPresets.day.sunPos);
    const bulbDay = new THREE.Color(0x566c56), bulbNight = new THREE.Color(0xffe6a0);
    const daylightWindow = new THREE.Color(0x466b6a);
    const darkWindow = new THREE.Color(0x172c37);
    const warmWindow = new THREE.Color(0xffcc87);
    const headlightNight = new THREE.Color(0xfff4c0), headlightDay = new THREE.Color(0x756d57);
    const taillightNight = new THREE.Color(0xff6453), taillightDay = new THREE.Color(0x80392d);
    let targetTime = lightingPresets.day;
    // Paleta construída para combinar com a linguagem verde-escuro/lima do URBS,
    // mas sem voltar à maquete anterior feita apenas de contornos transparentes.
    const material = (color, extras = {}) => new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({ color, roughness: .78, metalness: .09, ...extras });
    const asphalt = material(0x151d1b, { roughness: .97 });
    const darkAsphalt = material(0x1b2723, { roughness: .96 });
    const concrete = material(0x4b6053, { roughness: .95 });
    const pavement = material(0x809184, { roughness: .95 });
    const cream = material(0xc0c0a8, { roughness: .89 });
    const whiteLine = new THREE.MeshBasicMaterial({ color: 0xabb8a4, transparent: true, opacity: .65 });
    const crossing = new THREE.MeshBasicMaterial({ color: 0xe9e7d7, transparent: true, opacity: .94 });
    const grass = material(0x37583d, { roughness: 1 });
    const treeGreen = material(0x355d42, { roughness: 1, flatShading: true });
    const treeLime = material(0x6f9248, { roughness: 1, flatShading: true });
    const treeDark = material(0x284735, { roughness: 1, flatShading: true });
    const trunkMat = material(0x4d4931, { roughness: 1 });
    const darkMetal = material(0x2d3935, { metalness: .55, roughness: .52 });
    const glassBlue = material(0x678b89, { metalness: .42, roughness: .26 });
    const lightMetal = material(0xb2bcad, { metalness: .44, roughness: .38 });
    const neon = new THREE.MeshBasicMaterial({ color: 0xb8e954 });
    const roadGlow = new THREE.MeshBasicMaterial({ color: 0xb8e954, transparent: true, opacity: .16, depthWrite: false });
    const bulb = new THREE.MeshBasicMaterial({ color: 0xeaffc6 });
    const bulbSoft = new THREE.MeshBasicMaterial({ color: 0xeaffbd, transparent: true, opacity: .18, depthWrite: false });
    const towerMaterials = [
        material(0x748a7f, { roughness: .64, metalness: .17 }),
        material(0x9caa9b, { roughness: .84, metalness: .05 }),
        material(0x5b726d, { roughness: .57, metalness: .23 }),
        material(0x6d806d, { roughness: .92, metalness: .07 }),
        material(0xa0a38f, { roughness: .85, metalness: .03 }),
        material(0x4e645e, { roughness: .48, metalness: .23 })
    ];
    const windowLit = new THREE.MeshBasicMaterial({ color: 0xc9edaa });
    const windowCool = new THREE.MeshBasicMaterial({ color: 0x466b6a });
    const windowWarm = new THREE.MeshBasicMaterial({ color: 0xe0bb79 });
    const roofTop = material(0x384b43, { roughness: .91 });
    const city = new THREE.Group();
    scene.add(city);
    const box = new THREE.BoxGeometry(1, 1, 1);
    const plane = new THREE.PlaneGeometry(1, 1);
    const addBox = (parent, x, y, z, w, h, d, mat, cast = false) => {
        const mesh = new THREE.Mesh(box, mat);
        mesh.position.set(x, y, z);
        mesh.scale.set(w, h, d);
        mesh.castShadow = cast;
        mesh.receiveShadow = true;
        parent.add(mesh);
        return mesh;
    };
    let randomSeed = 27711;
    const rand = () => ((randomSeed = Math.imul(randomSeed, 1664525) + 1013904223 | 0) >>> 0) / 4294967296;
    // Plinto do bairro com espessura e contornos luminosos.
    addBox(city, 0, -1.2, 0, 68, 2.1, 68, material(0x122820, { roughness: 1 }), true);

    addBox(city, 0, -2.28, 0, 68, .04, 68, new THREE.MeshBasicMaterial({ color: 0x9abf58 }));
    addBox(city, 0, -2.47, 0, 70, .12, 70, material(0x152c20));
    const foundationRing = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.RingGeometry(37.7, 37.83, 112), new THREE.MeshBasicMaterial({ color: 0x99bd5e, transparent: true, opacity: .3, side: THREE.DoubleSide }));
    foundationRing.rotation.x = -Math.PI / 2;
    foundationRing.position.y = -2.45;
    city.add(foundationRing);
    // Vias e faixas: 4 x 4 quadras, com cruzamentos reconhecíveis.
    const roads = [-16, 0, 16];
    roads.forEach(c => {
        addBox(city, c, .033, 0, 4.9, .09, 64, asphalt);
        addBox(city, 0, .037, c, 64, .09, 4.9, asphalt);
    });
    // Pequenas faixas de divisão das pistas.
    const lineDashes = [];
    for (const c of roads) {
        for (let p = -30; p < 31; p += 4.7) {
            if (roads.some(k => Math.abs(p - k) < 3.7))
                continue;
            lineDashes.push({ x: c, z: p, rot: 0 }, { x: p, z: c, rot: 1 });
        }
    }
    const dashGeo = new THREE.BoxGeometry(.095, .014, 1.55);
    const dashMesh = new THREE.InstancedMesh(dashGeo, whiteLine, lineDashes.length);
    const temp = new THREE.Object3D();
    lineDashes.forEach((p, i) => { temp.position.set(p.x, .099, p.z); temp.rotation.set(0, p.rot ? Math.PI / 2 : 0, 0); temp.scale.set(1, 1, 1); temp.updateMatrix(); dashMesh.setMatrixAt(i, temp.matrix); });
    dashMesh.instanceMatrix.needsUpdate = true;
    city.add(dashMesh);
    // Travessias, ciclovias e quadrados de acessibilidade nos cruzamentos.
    const crossings = [];
    for (const x of roads)
        for (const z of roads) {
            for (let j = 0; j < 6; j++) {
                const p = -1.7 + j * .66;
                crossings.push({ x: x + p, z: z + 4.35, rot: 0 });
                crossings.push({ x: x + 4.35, z: z + p, rot: 1 });
            }
        }
    const stripeGeo = new THREE.BoxGeometry(.42, .018, 1.10);
    const stripeMesh = new THREE.InstancedMesh(stripeGeo, crossing, crossings.length);
    crossings.forEach((p, i) => { temp.position.set(p.x, .112, p.z); temp.rotation.set(0, p.rot ? Math.PI / 2 : 0, 0); temp.updateMatrix(); stripeMesh.setMatrixAt(i, temp.matrix); });
    stripeMesh.instanceMatrix.needsUpdate = true;

    // Bairros: fachadas sólidas, telhados, lojas, rampas e áreas verdes.
    const blocks = [-24, -8, 8, 24];

    const hits = [];

        lighting: { number: '02', name: 'ILUMINAÇÃO PÚBLICA', tag: 'SEGURANÇA E VISIBILIDADE', desc: 'Postes e pontos luminosos mostram a importância da visibilidade para o uso noturno dos espaços.', point: new THREE.Vector3(16, 1.6, -16), camera: [31, 20, -2], category: 'ILUMINAÇÃO' },
