                ambient: 0.78,
                hemi: 0.7,
                dir: 0.95,
                road: 0x6cd8ff,
                edge: 0x8cffea,
                build: 0x5dd7ff,
                clear: 0x06101b
            }
        };
        const t = themes[next] || themes.day;
        scene.fog.color.setHex(t.fog);
        ambient.intensity = t.ambient;
        hemi.intensity = t.hemi;
        dir.intensity = t.dir;
        base.material.color.setHex(t.clear);
        roadMats.forEach((m) => m.color.setHex(t.road));
        roadHighlightMats.forEach((m) => m.color.setHex(next === 'night' ? 0xb8e954 : 0xd4ff6b));
        edgeMats.forEach((m, idx) => m.color.setHex(idx % 4 === 0 ? t.edge : t.road));
        buildingMats.forEach((m) => {
            m.emissive.setHex(t.build);
            m.emissiveIntensity = next === 'night' ? 0.34 : next === 'dusk' ? 0.26 : 0.22;
            m.opacity = next === 'night' ? 0.34 : 0.28;
            m.color.setHex(next === 'night' ? 0x0d2440 : next === 'dusk' ? 0x2d2d23 : 0x0e392d);
        });
        setActiveButtons(buttonTime, next, 'holoTime');
        updateModeLabels();
    }
    function setMarkerState() {
        markerGroup.children.forEach((marker) => {
            const isActive = activePoint && marker.userData.point.id === activePoint.id;
            marker.userData.orb.material.color.setHex(isActive ? 0xf6ff88 : 0xb8e954);
            marker.userData.glow.material.opacity = isActive ? 0.36 : 0.24;
            marker.scale.setScalar(isActive ? 1.08 : 1);
        });
        roadHighlights.forEach((item) => {
            item.material.opacity = activePoint && item.id === activePoint.id ? 0.85 : 0;
        });
    }
    function fillCard(point) {
        activePoint = point;
        cardFields.category.textContent = point.category;
        cardFields.title.textContent = point.title;
        cardFields.description.textContent = point.description;
        cardFields.street.textContent = `Via: ${point.street}`;
        cardFields.coords.textContent = `Coordenadas: ${point.coords}`;
        cardFields.status.textContent = `Status: ${point.status}`;
        labelSelection.textContent = `${point.category} · ${point.street}`;
        card.hidden = false;
        setMarkerState();
    }
    const tempTarget = new THREE.Vector3();
    function moveCamera(position, target, onComplete) {
        const g = window.gsap;
        if (!g) {
            camera.position.copy(position);
            controls.target.copy(target);
            controls.update();
            onComplete?.();
            return;
        }
        g.killTweensOf(camera.position);
        g.killTweensOf(controls.target);
        g.to(camera.position, {
            x: position.x, y: position.y, z: position.z,
            duration: 1.55,
            ease: 'power3.inOut',
            onUpdate: () => controls.update(),
            onComplete
        });
        g.to(controls.target, {
            x: target.x, y: target.y, z: target.z,
            duration: 1.55,
            ease: 'power3.inOut',
            onUpdate: () => controls.update()
        });
    }
    function goAerial() {
        viewMode = 'aerial';
        controls.autoRotate = true;
        controls.maxPolarAngle = 1.28;
        controls.minPolarAngle = 0.75;
        streetPropsGroup.visible = false;
        moveCamera(aerialPose.position, aerialPose.target);
        updateModeLabels();
    }
    function goStreet(point = activePoint || points[0]) {
        if (!point)
            return;
        activePoint = point;
        fillCard(point);
        viewMode = 'street';
        controls.autoRotate = false;
        controls.maxPolarAngle = 1.5;
        controls.minPolarAngle = 0.7;
        streetPropsGroup.visible = true;
        streetPropsGroup.position.set(point.position.x, 0, point.position.z);
        streetPropsGroup.rotation.y = Math.atan2(point.roadPath.at(-1).z - point.roadPath[0].z, point.roadPath.at(-1).x - point.roadPath[0].x);
        const focus = point.position.clone().add(new THREE.Vector3(0, 0.55, 0));
        const direction = point.roadPath.at(-1).clone().sub(point.roadPath[0]).normalize();
        const side = new THREE.Vector3(-direction.z, 0, direction.x).multiplyScalar(1.4);
        const camPos = focus.clone().add(side).add(direction.clone().multiplyScalar(-2.0)).add(new THREE.Vector3(0, 1.55, 0));
        moveCamera(camPos, focus);
        updateModeLabels();
    }
    buttonTime.forEach((button) => button.addEventListener('click', () => applyTimeTheme(button.dataset.holoTime)));
    btnAerial?.addEventListener('click', goAerial);
    btnStreet?.addEventListener('click', () => goStreet(activePoint || points[0]));
    btnStreetAction?.addEventListener('click', () => goStreet(activePoint || points[0]));
    // Interaction
    function updatePointerPosition(event) {
        const rect = renderer.domElement.getBoundingClientRect();
        pointer.x = ((event.clientX - rect.left) / rect.width) * 2 - 1;
        pointer.y = -((event.clientY - rect.top) / rect.height) * 2 + 1;
    }
    renderer.domElement.addEventListener('pointermove', (event) => {
        updatePointerPosition(event);
        raycaster.setFromCamera(pointer, camera);
        const intersects = raycaster.intersectObjects(pickables, true);
        const marker = intersects[0]?.object?.parent?.userData?.point ? intersects[0].object.parent : intersects[0]?.object?.parent?.parent;
        if (marker && marker.userData && marker.userData.point) {
            hoverMarker = marker;
            renderer.domElement.style.cursor = 'pointer';
        }
        else {
            hoverMarker = null;
            renderer.domElement.style.cursor = 'grab';
        }
    });
    renderer.domElement.addEventListener('pointerdown', (event) => {
        updatePointerPosition(event);
        raycaster.setFromCamera(pointer, camera);
        const intersects = raycaster.intersectObjects(pickables, true);
        const found = intersects.find((item) => item.object.parent?.userData?.point || item.object.parent?.parent?.userData?.point);
        if (!found)
            return;
        const marker = found.object.parent?.userData?.point ? found.object.parent : found.object.parent?.parent;
        if (marker?.userData?.point) {
            fillCard(marker.userData.point);
            if (viewMode === 'street')
                goStreet(marker.userData.point);
        }
    });
    renderer.domElement.addEventListener('pointerleave', () => {
        hoverMarker = null;
        renderer.domElement.style.cursor = 'grab';
    });
    function resize() {
        const bounds = canvasHost.getBoundingClientRect();
        const width = Math.max(1, Math.round(bounds.width));
        const height = Math.max(1, Math.round(bounds.height));
        renderer.setSize(width, height, false);
        camera.aspect = width / height;
        camera.updateProjectionMatrix();
    }
    const ro = new ResizeObserver(resize);
    ro.observe(canvasHost);
    resize();
    // Default state
    applyTimeTheme('day');
    fillCard(points[0]);
    goAerial();
    const clock = new THREE.Clock();
    let raf = 0;
    function render() {
        raf = requestAnimationFrame(render);
        const t = clock.getElapsedTime();
        controls.update();
        markerGroup.children.forEach((marker, index) => {
            const baseScale = activePoint && marker.userData.point.id === activePoint.id ? 1.08 : 1;
            const pulse = 1 + Math.sin(t * 2.3 + index * 0.7) * 0.06;
            marker.userData.ring.scale.setScalar(pulse);
            marker.userData.ring.material.opacity = 0.36 + (Math.sin(t * 2.3 + index * 0.7) + 1) * 0.18;
            marker.userData.glow.scale.setScalar(1 + Math.sin(t * 1.8 + index) * 0.08);
            marker.scale.setScalar(baseScale * (hoverMarker === marker ? 1.08 : 1));
        });
        blockGroup.rotation.y = Math.sin(t * 0.16) * 0.02;
        roadMats.forEach((material, idx) => {
            material.opacity = 0.64 + Math.sin(t * 1.4 + idx) * 0.05;
        });
        renderer.render(scene, camera);
    }
    render();
    window.addEventListener('pagehide', () => {
        cancelAnimationFrame(raf);
        ro.disconnect();
        controls.dispose();
        renderer.dispose();
    }, { once: true });
})();


===== ARQUIVO: public\assets\js\urbs-v11.js =====
/* URBS V11 — safe, progressive enhancements. No scroll locking, no hidden sections. */
(() => {
    'use strict';
    const reduced = matchMedia('(prefers-reduced-motion: reduce)').matches;
    const menu = document.querySelector('.menu-toggle'), nav = document.querySelector('.nav');
    menu?.addEventListener('click', () => { const open = nav.classList.toggle('is-open'); menu.setAttribute('aria-expanded', String(open)); menu.setAttribute('aria-label', open ? 'Fechar menu' : 'Abrir menu'); });
    nav?.querySelectorAll('a').forEach(a => a.addEventListener('click', () => { nav.classList.remove('is-open'); menu?.setAttribute('aria-expanded', 'false'); }));
    const bar = document.querySelector('.progress span');
    let scheduled = false;
    const onScroll = () => { if (scheduled)
        return; scheduled = true; requestAnimationFrame(() => { const max = document.documentElement.scrollHeight - innerHeight; if (bar)
        bar.style.transform = `scaleX(${Math.max(0, Math.min(1, max ? scrollY / max : 0))})`; scheduled = false; }); };
    addEventListener('scroll', onScroll, { passive: true });
    onScroll();
    const counter = document.getElementById('local-record-count');
    if (counter && window.UrbsData) {
        try {
            counter.textContent = String(UrbsData.getStoredReports().length);
        }
        catch {
            counter.textContent = '0';
        }
    }
    // Galeria "ONDE VOCÊ OLHA": faixa automática, suave e ininterrupta.
    // A rolagem vertical da página permanece nativa.
    initStoryCarousel();
    function initStoryCarousel() {
        const viewport = document.getElementById('story-viewport');
        const track = viewport?.querySelector('.story-track');
        if (!viewport || !track)
            return;
        const originals = [...track.querySelectorAll('.story-card')];
        if (originals.length < 2)
            return;
        // Duplicar exatamente uma sequência evita voltar abruptamente ao início.
        // As cópias são apenas visuais e não fazem parte da navegação por teclado.
        originals.forEach(card => {
            const clone = card.cloneNode(true);
            clone.setAttribute('aria-hidden', 'true');
            clone.setAttribute('tabindex', '-1');
            clone.querySelectorAll('a,button,input,[tabindex]').forEach(el => el.setAttribute('tabindex', '-1'));
            track.appendChild(clone);
        });
        viewport.classList.add('is-carousel');
        const prefersLessMotion = matchMedia('(prefers-reduced-motion: reduce)').matches;
        const SPEED = 42; // pixels/segundo: moderado, sem saltar de card em card.
        let period = 0, offset = 0, previous = 0, frameId = 0, visible = true, dragging = false;
        let pointerStartX = 0, pointerStartOffset = 0, moved = false, pauseUntil = 0;
        const measure = () => {
            const first = originals[0], firstDuplicate = track.children[originals.length];
            period = firstDuplicate.offsetLeft - first.offsetLeft;
            if (period > 0) {
                offset = ((offset % period) + period) % period;
                viewport.scrollLeft = offset;
            }
        };
        const tick = now => {
            const delta = previous ? Math.min((now - previous) / 1000, .05) : 0;
            previous = now;
            if (!prefersLessMotion && visible && !document.hidden && !dragging && now > pauseUntil && period > 0) {
                offset = (offset + SPEED * delta) % period;
                viewport.scrollLeft = offset;
            }
            frameId = requestAnimationFrame(tick);
        };
        // Arraste com mouse sem interromper definitivamente o movimento automático.
        viewport.addEventListener('pointerdown', e => {
            if (e.pointerType !== 'mouse' || e.button !== 0)
                return;
            dragging = true;
            moved = false;
            pointerStartX = e.clientX;
            pointerStartOffset = offset;
            viewport.classList.add('dragging');
        });
        viewport.addEventListener('pointermove', e => {
            if (!dragging || !period)
                return;
            const dx = e.clientX - pointerStartX;
            if (Math.abs(dx) > 5)
                moved = true;
            if (moved) {
                offset = ((pointerStartOffset - dx) % period + period) % period;
                viewport.scrollLeft = offset;
            }
        });
        const endDrag = () => {
            if (!dragging)
                return;
            dragging = false;
            viewport.classList.remove('dragging');
            pauseUntil = performance.now() + 850;
            previous = 0;
        };
        window.addEventListener('pointerup', endDrag);
        window.addEventListener('pointercancel', endDrag);
        viewport.addEventListener('click', e => {
            if (moved) {
                e.preventDefault();
                e.stopPropagation();
                moved = false;
            }
        }, true);
        viewport.addEventListener('focusin', () => { pauseUntil = Number.POSITIVE_INFINITY; });
        viewport.addEventListener('focusout', e => {
            if (!viewport.contains(e.relatedTarget)) {
                pauseUntil = performance.now() + 1200;
                previous = 0;
            }
        });
        // Teclado continua navegável mesmo sem setas visíveis.
        viewport.addEventListener('keydown', e => {
            if (e.key === 'ArrowRight' || e.key === 'ArrowLeft') {
                e.preventDefault();
                if (!period)
                    return;
                const direction = e.key === 'ArrowRight' ? 1 : -1;
                offset = ((offset + direction * (originals[0].offsetWidth + 20)) % period + period) % period;
                viewport.scrollLeft = offset;
                pauseUntil = performance.now() + 1800;
            }
        });
        const observer = ('IntersectionObserver' in window) ? new IntersectionObserver(entries => {
            visible = entries[0].isIntersecting;
            previous = 0;
        }, { threshold: .01 }) : null;
        observer?.observe(viewport);
        const resizer = ('ResizeObserver' in window) ? new ResizeObserver(measure) : null;
        resizer?.observe(viewport);
        resizer?.observe(track);
        addEventListener('resize', measure, { passive: true });
        measure();
        frameId = requestAnimationFrame(tick);
        addEventListener('pagehide', () => {
            cancelAnimationFrame(frameId);
            observer?.disconnect();
            resizer?.disconnect();
        }, { once: true });
    }
    // V12.1: hero without cursor-reactive effect. Keep atmosphere static and clean.
    const hero = document.querySelector('.hero');
    if (hero) {
        hero.style.setProperty('--hero-x', '50%');
        hero.style.setProperty('--hero-y', '34%');
    }
    if (!reduced) {
        const impact = document.querySelector('.impact');
        if (impact) {
            let queued = false;
            const update = () => {
                queued = false;
                const r = impact.getBoundingClientRect();
                const progress = Math.max(0, Math.min(1, (innerHeight - r.top) / (innerHeight + r.height)));
                impact.style.setProperty('--impact-zoom', (1.12 - progress * .12).toFixed(3));
            };
            const request = () => { if (!queued) {
                queued = true;
                requestAnimationFrame(update);
            } };
            addEventListener('scroll', request, { passive: true });
            addEventListener('resize', request, { passive: true });
            request();
        }
    }
    // GSAP is optional; never set opacity:0 or position:fixed on sections.
    if (window.gsap && !reduced) {
        try {
            if (!document.documentElement.classList.contains('urbs-arrival')) {
                gsap.fromTo('.hero-content', { y: 20 }, { y: 0, duration: 1.25, ease: 'power3.out' });
            }
            gsap.to('.hero-photo', { scale: 1.17, scrollTrigger: undefined, duration: 18, repeat: -1, yoyo: true, ease: 'sine.inOut' });
        }
        catch (e) {
            console.warn('URBS: animação de abertura indisponível', e);
        }
    }
    // Hover animado nos títulos, acessível e sem bloquear a rolagem vertical.
    // Manter o texto semântico no aria-label e caracteres decorativos aria-hidden.
    if (!reduced && matchMedia('(hover: hover) and (pointer: fine)').matches) {
        document.querySelectorAll('main h1, main h2').forEach(heading => {
            const label = heading.innerText.replace(/\s+/g, ' ').trim();
            if (!label)
                return;
            heading.setAttribute('aria-label', label);
            heading.classList.add('title-jump');
            const walker = document.createTreeWalker(heading, NodeFilter.SHOW_TEXT);
            const nodes = [];
            while (walker.nextNode())
                nodes.push(walker.currentNode);
            nodes.forEach(node => {
                const frag = document.createDocumentFragment();
                // Palavras inteiras evitam quebrar títulos no meio de uma palavra.
                const tokens = node.textContent.match(/\s+|\S+/g) || [];
                tokens.forEach(token => {
                    if (/^\s+$/.test(token)) {
                        frag.appendChild(document.createTextNode(token));
                        return;
                    }
                    const word = document.createElement('span');
                    word.className = 'jump-word';
                    word.setAttribute('aria-hidden', 'true');
                    for (const ch of token) {
                        const letter = document.createElement('span');
                        letter.className = 'jump-letter';
                        letter.textContent = ch;
                        word.appendChild(letter);
                    }
                    frag.appendChild(word);
                });
                node.replaceWith(frag);
            });
            heading.addEventListener('pointerover', event => {
                const letter = event.target.closest('.jump-letter');
                if (!letter || !heading.contains(letter) || letter === event.relatedTarget)
                    return;
                if (window.gsap) {
                    gsap.killTweensOf(letter);
                    gsap.fromTo(letter, { y: 0, rotate: 0, scale: 1 }, { y: -13, rotate: (Math.random() - .5) * 12, scale: 1.07, duration: .22, ease: 'power2.out', onComplete: () => gsap.to(letter, { y: 0, rotate: 0, scale: 1, duration: .65, ease: 'bounce.out', clearProps: 'transform' }) });
                }
                else {
                    letter.classList.remove('jump-active');
                    void letter.offsetWidth;
                    letter.classList.add('jump-active');
                }
            });
        });
    }
})();


===== ARQUIVO: public\assets\js\visual-effects.js =====
/** URBS visual layer. Progressive enhancement: core pages work without CDNs. */
const reduceMotion = matchMedia('(prefers-reduced-motion: reduce)');
const mobile = matchMedia('(max-width: 760px)');
const cleanup = [];
const safe = (label, callback) => { try {
    callback();
}
catch (error) {
    console.warn(`[URBS visual] ${label}`, error);
} };
function init() {
    const active = !reduceMotion.matches;
    if (window.Swiper)
        safe('Swiper', () => new Swiper('.urbs-swiper', {
            slidesPerView: 1.1, spaceBetween: 14, grabCursor: true,
            breakpoints: { 640: { slidesPerView: 2.1, spaceBetween: 18 }, 1050: { slidesPerView: 3, spaceBetween: 20 } },
            navigation: { nextEl: '.showcase-next', prevEl: '.showcase-prev' },
            pagination: { el: '.showcase-controls .swiper-pagination', type: 'bullets', clickable: true }
        }));
    if (!active)
        return;
    // Native page scroll is intentional on the homepage. Lenis can interfere with
    // wheel/touch events or other scroll-driven libraries, especially after CDN failures.
    // We keep the library available but do not intercept vertical scrolling.
    if (window.VanillaTilt && !mobile.matches)
        safe('Tilt', () => {
            VanillaTilt.init(document.querySelectorAll('.category-card'), { max: 5, speed: 450, scale: 1.015, glare: false, perspective: 1200 });
            cleanup.push(() => document.querySelectorAll('.category-card').forEach(el => el.vanillaTilt?.destroy()));
        });
    // Critical content must be visible without scroll event observers.
    // AOS and scroll-triggered reveal effects remain disabled until verified separately.
    if (window.gsap)
        safe('GSAP', () => {
            gsap.fromTo('.hero-copy > *', { y: 15 }, { y: 0, duration: .7, stagger: .08, ease: 'power2.out', clearProps: 'all' });
        });
    // Vanta uses THREE; keep it confined to the showcase, never overlay the Leaflet map.
    if (window.VANTA?.NET && window.THREE && !mobile.matches)
        safe('Vanta', () => {
            const effect = VANTA.NET({ el: '.visual-lab-network', THREE: window.THREE, mouseControls: false,
                touchControls: false, gyroControls: false, minHeight: 200, minWidth: 200,
                color: 0x91b87d, backgroundColor: 0x0b2119, points: 8, maxDistance: 19, spacing: 21, showDots: true });
            cleanup.push(() => effect.destroy());
        });
    if (window.THREE && !mobile.matches)
        safe('Three.js', () => {
            const host = document.querySelector('.hero-atmosphere');
            if (!host)
                return;
            const scene = new THREE.Scene();
            const camera = new THREE.PerspectiveCamera(55, 1, .1, 100);
            camera.position.z = 11;
            const renderer = new THREE.WebGLRenderer({ alpha: true, antialias: false, powerPreference: 'low-power' });
            renderer.setPixelRatio(Math.min(devicePixelRatio, 1.5));
            host.appendChild(renderer.domElement);
            const count = 85, coords = new Float32Array(count * 3);
            for (let i = 0; i < count; i++) {
                coords[i * 3] = (Math.random() - .5) * 19;
                coords[i * 3 + 1] = (Math.random() - .5) * 10;
                coords[i * 3 + 2] = (Math.random() - .5) * 7;
            }
            const geo = new THREE.BufferGeometry();
            geo.setAttribute('position', new THREE.BufferAttribute(coords, 3));
            const mat = new THREE.PointsMaterial({ color: 0xa8d589, size: .035, transparent: true, opacity: .75 });
            const points = new THREE.Points(geo, mat);
            scene.add(points);
            const resize = () => { const r = host.getBoundingClientRect(); renderer.setSize(Math.max(r.width, 1), Math.max(r.height, 1)); camera.aspect = Math.max(r.width, 1) / Math.max(r.height, 1); camera.updateProjectionMatrix(); };
            resize();
            window.addEventListener('resize', resize);
            let id, t = 0;
            const draw = () => { t += .001; points.rotation.y = t; renderer.render(scene, camera); id = requestAnimationFrame(draw); };
            draw();
            cleanup.push(() => { cancelAnimationFrame(id); window.removeEventListener('resize', resize); geo.dispose(); mat.dispose(); renderer.dispose(); renderer.domElement.remove(); });
        });
    // Motion can be re-enabled for hover interactions, but not visibility-critical reveals.
    // Lottie: small vector status indicator, no remote animation asset needed.
    if (window.lottie)
        safe('Lottie', () => {
            const dot = document.querySelector('.hero .status-dot');
            if (!dot)
                return;
            const host = document.createElement('span');
            host.className = 'urbs-lottie-dot';
            dot.replaceWith(host);
            const player = lottie.loadAnimation({ container: host, renderer: 'svg', loop: true, autoplay: true,
                animationData: { v: '5.7.4', fr: 30, ip: 0, op: 60, w: 40, h: 40, nm: 'pulse', ddd: 0, assets: [], layers: [{ ddd: 0, ind: 1, ty: 4, nm: 'dot', sr: 1, ks: { o: { a: 0, k: 100 }, r: { a: 0, k: 0 }, p: { a: 0, k: [20, 20, 0] }, a: { a: 0, k: [0, 0, 0] }, s: { a: 1, k: [{ t: 0, s: [65, 65, 100] }, { t: 30, s: [110, 110, 100] }, { t: 60, s: [65, 65, 100] }] } }, shapes: [{ ty: 'el', p: { a: 0, k: [0, 0] }, s: { a: 0, k: [18, 18] }, nm: 'circle' }, { ty: 'fl', c: { a: 0, k: [.7, .94, .42, 1] }, o: { a: 0, k: 100 }, r: 1 }], ao: 0, ip: 0, op: 60, st: 0, bm: 0 }] } });
            cleanup.push(() => player.destroy());
        });
}
if (document.readyState === 'loading')
    document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', init, { once: true });
else
    init();
window.addEventListener('pagehide', () => cleanup.forEach(fn => safe('cleanup', fn)), { once: true });
